Result: 实现了对所有Cressie-Read阶k>1的紧样本复杂度界：可实现情形为max{1/ε, ρ^{1/(k-1)}/ε^{k_*}}·(d+log δ^{-1})，不可知情形为max{1/ε², ρ^{1/(k-1)}/ε^{k_*∨2}}·(d+log δ^{-1})。定理表明固定ρ>0时，可实现情形的ε依赖从ε^{-1}变为ε^{-k_*}；不可知情形下，1<k<2时从ε^{-2}变为ε^{-k_*}，k≥2时保持指数2但出现非平凡的ρ依赖。同时闭合了此前χ²-散度情形的上下界间隙，并确保ρ→0时正确恢复标准PAC学习率。

Conclusion: 论文建立了Cressie-Read散度族下分布鲁棒PAC学习的完整样本复杂度理论，揭示了鲁棒性放大与统计估计之间的交互机制，证明了ERM在该框架下的最优性，并统一了此前分散的结果。该工作为分布鲁棒学习提供了与经典PAC理论相衔接的紧致刻画。

Abstract: We study distributionally robust PAC learning for the $0$--$1$-loss, where adversarial perturbations of the data distribution are constrained by a Cressie--Read divergence of order $k>1$ and radius $ρ\geq 0$. For hypothesis classes with VC dimension $d$, we establish realizable and agnostic sample-complexity bounds tight up to constant and logarithmic factors, respectively; ordinary empirical risk minimization attains both rates up to logarithmic factors. For target accuracy $\varepsilon\in(0,1)$ and confidence $δ\in(0,1)$, their respective orders are \[ \max\!\left\{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}, \frac{ρ^{\frac 1{k-1}}}{\varepsilon^{k_\star}} \right\}\cdot(d+\log δ^{-1}) \qquad\text{and}\qquad \max\!\left\{\frac{1}{\varepsilon^2}, \frac{ρ^{\frac1{k-1}}}{\varepsilon^{k_\star\vee 2}} \right\}\cdot(d+\log δ^{-1}), \] where $k_\star={k}/{(k-1)}$. For every fixed $ρ>0$, robustness changes the realizable $\varepsilon$-dependence from $\varepsilon^{-1}$ to $\varepsilon^{-k_\star}$ as $\varepsilon\downarrow0$. In the agnostic case, for $1<k<2$, robustness changes the $\varepsilon$-dependence from $\varepsilon^{-2}$ to $\varepsilon^{-k_\star}$, whereas for $k\geq2$ the exponent remains the classical $2$, with nontrivial $ρ$-dependence.
  Building on the known scalar reduction of robust $0$--$1$ risk to ordinary classification error, our analysis reveals a scale-sensitive interaction between the statistical estimation of classification error and its amplification by robustness, sharply explaining the transition in the agnostic rate. We extend the previously studied $χ^2$-divergence case to every Cressie--Read order $k>1$, close its upper--lower gaps, and recover standard PAC learning rates as $ρ\to0$, unlike previous bounds that fail to interpolate correctly in this limit.

</details>


### [66] [Personalized Federated Sparse Adaptation of Time-Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04695)
*Priyanka Nihalchandani,Naman Srivastava,Varun Ojha,Pandarasamy Arjunan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出面向建筑能耗预测的个性化联邦稀疏自适应框架，在预训练时间序列基础模型之后引入异构时序混合专家（MoE）适配器，通过序列级路由将每个168小时上下文窗口映射到针对周期性、长期交互、局部变化、趋势残差和多分辨率行为特化的专家子集。在50栋建筑和三个TSFM骨干上验证，个性化方案始终优于全局和本地MoE，且最佳稀疏自适应策略因骨干和指标而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑能耗预测中电表数据是隐私的、分布式的且高度非IID的，联邦学习适配TSFM具有吸引力。但单一参数共享策略无法适用于所有预训练TSFM或建筑客户：完全共享适配器会抑制建筑特有的时序行为，而完全本地化适配又丢弃了跨建筑迁移能力，因此需要一种既能个性化又能保持迁移的适配方案。

Method: 提出个性化联邦稀疏自适应框架，在预训练TSFM表示后放置异构时序混合专家（MoE）适配器。使用序列级路由器将每个168小时上下文窗口映射到top-k专家子集，这些专家分别专攻周期性、长期交互、局部变化、趋势残差结构和多分辨率行为。对比了全局FL、本地训练和个性化FL变体，并探索全局共享或客户端私有的专家库两种设置。

Result: 在50栋建筑和三个TSFM骨干上，个性化方法始终优于Global FL-MoE和Local MoE。但最佳稀疏自适应策略因骨干网络和评估指标而异。路由行为揭示了客户端级专家特化、专家集中以及跨骨干的近均匀路由现象。

Conclusion: 联邦TSFM适配应同时考虑客户端感知和骨干感知（client-aware and backbone-aware）。不同预训练TSFM和建筑客户端需要不同的稀疏适配策略，单一策略难以通用，个性化联邦MoE是有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated adaptation of time-series foundation models (TSFMs) is attractive for building energy forecasting because meter data are private, distributed, and highly non-IID. However, a single parameter-sharing strategy is unlikely to serve all pretrained TSFMs or building clients: fully shared adapters can suppress building-specific temporal behavior, while fully local adaptation discards cross-building transfer. We propose a personalized federated sparse adaptation framework with a heterogeneous temporal mixture-of-experts (MoE) adapter placed after the pretrained TSFM representation. A sequence-level router maps each 168-hour context window to a top-$k$ subset of experts specialized for periodicity, long-range interactions, local variation, trend-residual structure, and multi-resolution behavior. We compare global FL, local training, and personalized FL variants with globally shared or client-private expert banks. Across 50 buildings and three TSFM backbones, personalization consistently outperforms Global FL-MoE and Local MoE, while the best sparse-adaptation strategy varies by backbone and metric. Routing behavior further reveals client-level expert specialization, expert concentration, and near-uniform routing across backbones, showing that federated TSFM adaptation should be both client-aware and backbone-aware.

</details>


### [67] [Design Choices That Matter: A Functional ANOVA Analysis for Remote Sensing Multi-Label Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04702)
*Maryam Gholami Shiri,Eva Tuba,Sašo Džeroski,Tome Eftimov,Ana Nikolikj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文使用功能方差分析（fANOVA）系统量化深度学习模型设计选择（架构、微调策略、学习策略、初始化）对遥感图像多标签分类性能变异的贡献，并在7个数据集上构建设计选择敏感性画像，通过层次聚类发现数据集可按对设计决策的响应自然分组，且与尺度、空间分辨率和标签空间复杂度等内在属性相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基准测试仅给出模型排名，无法泛化到新数据集。本研究旨在超越排名，系统量化各设计选择及其交互对性能变异的贡献，揭示数据集内在属性与设计选择敏感性之间的关系。

Method: 采用功能方差分析（fANOVA）对48个和20个深度学习模型进行两项实证分析，覆盖网络架构、微调策略、学习策略和初始化等设计维度；在7个多标签遥感图像数据集上计算设计选择敏感性画像，构建数据集元表征，并应用层次聚类分析数据集的自然分组。

Result: 发现数据集根据对设计决策的响应自然分组，且与数据集内在属性（尺度、空间分辨率、标签空间复杂度）强相关。大规模数据集中微调策略和架构主导性能；数据有限场景下初始化起决定性作用；中等规模场景下架构与学习策略的交互是性能的关键因素。

Conclusion: 设计选择对性能的影响随数据集规模与特性而变化，揭示了不同数据体制下的主导因素，为遥感图像多标签分类中根据数据集特性选择合适设计策略提供了系统性指导。

Abstract: Benchmarking deep learning (DL) models for multi-label classification (MLC) of remote sensing images (RSI) typically yields rankings that do not generalize beyond the evaluated datasets. In this work, we move beyond rankings by employing functional analysis of variance (fANOVA) to systematically quantify the contributions of individual design choices and their interactions to performance variability. We conduct two empirical analyses covering 48 and 20 DL models, respectively, spanning design choices such as network architecture, fine-tuning strategy, learning strategy, and initialization. By applying fANOVA across seven MLC RSI datasets, we construct dataset meta-representations that capture design-choice sensitivity profiles. Hierarchical clustering of these meta-representations reveals that datasets naturally group according to how they respond to design decisions, with patterns strongly linked to intrinsic dataset properties such as scale, spatial resolution, and label space complexity. Our findings show that for large-scale datasets, fine-tuning strategy and architecture are dominant factors, while in data-limited regimes, initialization becomes decisive. For intermediate regimes, the interaction between architecture and learning strategy governs performance.

</details>


### [68] [Benchmarking Deep Learning Models for Dense Event Classification of Offshore Wind Infrastructure in Sentinel-1 Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04706)
*Thorsten Hoeser,Felix Bachofer,Claudia Kuenzer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出十种深度学习模型训练变体用于Sentinel-1 SAR时间序列的密集分类，以改进海上风电基础设施的基于规则的事件分类。监督式BiLSTM表现最佳（AUC从0.7853提升至0.8509），结合标签转移最小化集成进一步改进。基于改进标签在全球范围内隔离了单个风机的部署阶段，报告中国（84天）、欧盟（242天）和英国（258天）的中位部署时长，并揭示了部署驱动因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上风电基础设施的生命周期监测（特别是部署阶段）对利益相关者的决策至关重要。Sentinel-1 SAR任务产生大量数据档案，但将这些高容量档案转化为信息需要能够从全球范围的密集时间序列中自动提取单一事件标签的算法，现有基于规则的方法尚可改进。

Method: 系统比较了十种深度学习模型训练变体用于Sentinel-1海上风电基础设施时间序列的密集分类，包括LSTM、Transformer和全连接模型变体，分别具有单调时、单向和双向上下文感知，每种都含/不含自监督预训练。同时提出了将BiLSTM预测与现有基线标签相结合的标签转移最小化集成策略。

Result: 监督式BiLSTM表现最佳，将目标AUC从规则基线的0.7853提高到0.8509，完全匹配率从0.3508提高到0.5063。结合BiLSTM预测与基线标签的集成进一步提升了与测试数据的一致性。基于改进标签的全球部署阶段分析报告了中位部署时长：中国84天、欧盟242天、英国258天（2016-01-01至2025-03-31），并识别出部署驱动因素，包括法律监管（如补贴）和环境条件，在多个空间尺度上表现清晰。

Conclusion: 深度学习模型，尤其是带双向上下文的BiLSTM，能显著优于基于规则的事件分类方法。将模型预测与规则基线集成的策略进一步提高了标签质量，使全球尺度上的部署阶段隔离和区域/子区域分析成为可能，揭示了影响部署时长的法规与环境驱动因素。

Abstract: Monitoring of offshore wind energy infrastructure life cycles, especially during the deployment phase, is an important contribution for stakeholders to make informed decisions in a phase of increasing deployment activities. ESA's Sentinel-1 Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) mission produces large data archives that enable the global monitoring of offshore wind infrastructure. Turning these high-volume archives into information requires algorithms that automatically extract single event labels from dense time series at a global scale. In this study, we present a structured comparison of ten deep learning model-training variants for the dense classification of Sentinel-1 based offshore wind infrastructure time series, aiming to advance rule-based event classification of this task. We trained LSTM, Transformer, and fully connected model variants with monotemporal, unidirectional, and bidirectional context awareness, each with and without self-supervised pretraining. Among these, the supervised BiLSTM performs best, raising the target AUC score from 0.7853 for the rule-based baseline to 0.8509, and the perfect match rate from 0.3508 to 0.5063. Combining the BiLSTM predictions with the existing baseline labels in a label-transition-minimising ensemble further improves agreement with the test data. Using these improved labels, we isolate the deployment phase of individual turbines at a global scale and conduct a regional and subregional analysis covering 2016-01-01 to 2025-03-31, reporting median deployment durations of 84 d (China), 242 d (EU), and 258 d (UK). Deployment-related drivers, including legal regulations such as subsidies, and environmental conditions, emerge clearly from the analysed results across multiple spatial scales.

</details>


### [69] [A 6G Integrated Sensing and Communication Framework for Railway Intrusion Detection and Collision Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04710)
*Ajeet Kumar Yadav,Sankaran Balasubramaniam,Aritra Chatterjee,Vinod Aduru,Yogesh Simmhan,Pandarasamy Arjunan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对3GPP Release 19中定义的"铁路入侵检测"ISAC用例，利用Sionna无线电模拟器和3D渲染铁路环境生成了22,695个CSI矩阵，并开发了3D CNN与BiLSTM结合的机器学习模型，在合成数据上实现了99.57%的入侵检测准确率和0.4240的综合MAE（位置、速度、碰撞时间预测），展示了基于CSI的ISAC感知在铁路入侵检测中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ISAC是下一代无线网络的关键范式，3GPP Release 19确定了32个ISAC用例，其中铁路入侵检测至关重要——野生动物等入侵者进入铁轨会带来严重的碰撞风险，需要可靠的实时感知方案。

Method: 使用3D渲染铁路环境和Sionna无线电模拟器生成22,695个含地面真值的CSI矩阵；构建3D CNN与BiLSTM的混合深度学习模型，用于检测轨道危险区内的入侵者并估计其相对火车的实时位置、速度和碰撞时间。

Result: 在平衡测试集上达到99.57%的入侵检测准确率；位置、速度和碰撞时间预测的综合MAE为0.4240；代码库已公开。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于CSI的ISAC感知结合机器学习能够为铁路入侵检测提供可靠的解决方案，验证了该方法在实际ISAC场景中的可行性。

Abstract: Integrated Sensing and Communication (ISAC) combines sensing and communication to efficiently utilize wireless resources and is emerging as a key paradigm for next-generation wireless networks. By leveraging the wide bandwidth, high frequencies, and massive antenna arrays of 5G-Advanced and 6G systems, ISAC enables physical-layer sensing using Channel State Information (CSI). The 3rd Generation Partnership Project (3GPP) Release 19 identifies 32 potential ISAC use cases, with particular emphasis on detecting and tracking moving objects. In this work, we address the Sensing for Railway Intrusion Detection use case, where intruders, including wildlife, entering a railway track can pose serious collision risks. We generated 22,695 CSI matrices with corresponding ground truth using a 3D-rendered railway environment and the Sionna radio simulator. We developed a machine learning model combining a three-dimensional Convolutional Neural Network (3D CNN) and Bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory (BiLSTM) network to detect intruders in the track danger zone and estimate their real-time position relative to the train, velocity, and time to collision. On synthetic CSI data, the model achieves 99.57% intruder-detection accuracy on a balanced test set and a combined Mean Absolute Error (MAE) of 0.4240 for position, velocity, and time-to-collision prediction. These results demonstrate the potential of CSI-based ISAC sensing with machine learning for reliable railway intrusion detection. The complete codebase for CSI generation, preprocessing, and model development is publicly available at https://github.com/EdgeIntelligenceLab/6g-isac-railway-intrusion-detection.

</details>


### [70] [Attention, Anomalies! Handling Attention Layers in Unsupervised Federated Outlier Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04753)
*Mihailo Ilić,Miloš Savić,Vladimir Kurbalija,Mirjana Ivanović,Giancarlo Fortino,Dušan Jakovetić*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对联邦学习场景中记忆增强自编码器（MemAE）缺乏专门聚合技术的问题，提出了新的引导式聚合方法，在非IID和非均衡数据集上表现出更强的鲁棒性，特别适用于无监督异常检测任务，即使对于浅层自编码器也能提升性能，适合资源受限环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力层是现代模型的核心组件，在记忆增强自编码器（MemAE）中已被证明在集中式学习场景下对无监督表示学习和异常检测有效。然而，在联邦学习（FL）场景下，MemAE模型缺乏专门设计的聚合技术，现有方法无法有效应对非IID、数据不均衡等联邦学习挑战。

Method: 作者深入分析了MemAE的架构特性，提出新颖的引导式（guided）聚合方案，专门针对MemAE在联邦场景中的参数聚合进行优化设计，并在非IID数据集及多边缘节点、数据分布不均衡的环境中进行验证。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的聚合方案在无监督异常检测任务中比现有方法具有更强的鲁棒性，即使在数据分布不均衡、大量边缘节点的环境中表现良好，且能够提升浅层自编码器的性能，使其适用于资源受限场景。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了联邦学习中MemAE模型专用聚合技术的空白，为在分布式、数据异构环境中部署基于注意力机制的自编码器提供了有效方案，拓展了MemAE在资源受限和隐私保护场景中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Attention layers are the backbone of today's most powerful and impactful models. Models with multi-million and billion parameters rely on contextual knowledge provided by attention layers. However, their use goes well beyond just being the core component of large language models. One particularly interesting application is in Memory Augmented Autoencoders (MemAE), specifically for unsupervised representation learning in outlier detection tasks. It was shown that attention helps these models be more effective in centralized learning scenarios. Our work aims to address the lack of specialized aggregation techniques in Federated Learning (FL) when it comes to MemAE models. In this paper we analyze the intricacies of the architecture behind Memory Augmented Autoencoders, and propose novel, guided approaches to effectively aggregate these models in federated scenarios. We demonstrate our approach on non-IID datasets and show that these novel aggregation schemes are more robust when dealing with numerous edge nodes in environments with unbalanced datasets, specifically for unsupervised anomaly detection scenarios. This approach improves the performance of even very shallow autoencoders, allowing them to be used in resource constrained environments.

</details>


### [71] [IMFACT: Counterfactual Explanations for Time Series via Intrinsic Mode Function Substitution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04777)
*Udo Schlegel,Julian Rakuschek,Thomas Seidl,Andreas Holzinger,Tobias Schreck,Javier Del Ser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出IMFACT框架，一种基于经验模态分解（EMD）的模型无关反事实解释生成方法，通过在IMF分解空间中逐步替换信号的固有模态函数（IMFs）来生成物理上合理的反事实时间序列，并在两个UCR基准数据集上超越了两种主流基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 振荡信号（如振动）中的类别判别信息集中在特定频带，但在原始特征空间中扰动这些信号进行反事实分析会破坏其时域结构，产生物理上不可信的结果。因此需要一种能在保留信号物理结构的前提下生成合理反事实解释的方法。

Method: IMFACT框架将输入信号通过经验模态分解（EMD）拆分为多个固有模态函数（IMFs），然后选择部分IMFs，逐步用最近异类邻居（NUN）的对应IMFs替换，直到分类器翻转至目标类别。框架评估了六种IMF选择策略以及一种多NUN循环扩展方案。

Result: 在两个UCR基准数据集（FaultDetectionA和FruitFlies）上，基于方差的三NUN策略在可靠性和合理性指标上优于两种主流基线技术；而三NUN循环策略在两个数据集上均取得了最佳的邻近性（proximity）表现。

Conclusion: 在EMD分解空间中生成反事实解释，能够有效保留信号的时域结构和物理合理性，方差驱动的IMF选择策略与多NUN循环机制是提升反事实解释质量的有效设计。

Abstract: Oscillatory signals, such as vibration, carry class-discriminative information in specific frequency bands; perturbing them in raw feature space for counterfactual analysis easily destroys their temporal structure and produces physically implausible results. In this work, we introduce IMFACT (IMF-based counterfACTuals), a model-agnostic framework for generating plausible counterfactual explanations for time series classifiers that operates in the decomposition space of Empirical Mode Decomposition. An input signal is split into Intrinsic Mode Functions (IMFs), and selected IMFs are progressively substituted with those of a Nearest Unlike Neighbour (NUN) until the classifier flips to the target class. We evaluate six IMF-selection strategies and a multi-NUN cycling extension on two UCR benchmarks (FaultDetectionA, FruitFlies). The variance-based strategy with three NUNs outperforms two prominent baseline techniques on reliability and plausibility metrics, while cycling across three NUNs yields the best proximity across both datasets.

</details>


### [72] [Continual-Learning Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Parameterized Partial Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04778)
*Xujia Chen,Xinyue Hu,Letian Chen,Yi Liu,Wenhui Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CL-PINN，一种基于持续学习的物理信息神经网络，将不同参数下的PDE实例视为序列化的相关任务，结合贝叶斯优化主动参数选择、任务级动态损失加权、稀疏物理约束回放和可选参数子网络，在无需观测数据且计算资源受限的条件下，实现对参数化PDE解的高精度、均衡且可泛化的学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数化PINN（ParamPINN）存在三个核心问题：训练效率低下、不同参数下的精度不均衡、以及对有限采样参数任务的过拟合导致对未采样参数的泛化能力差。这些问题限制了其在较大参数域上的实际应用。

Method: CL-PINN将不同参数值的PDE实例视为相关任务并按序列学习，组合了四种关键技术：基于贝叶斯优化的主动参数选择（减少目标损失查询次数）、任务级动态损失加权（改善任务间精度均衡）、稀疏物理约束回放（缓解对先前任务的遗忘）、以及可选参数子网络（改善任务分配）。整个方法无需观测数据，并在有限计算资源下运行。

Result: 在五个基准上（一个连续函数和四个参数化PDE）的多种子评估显示：贝叶斯选择相较网格贪心搜索大幅减少目标损失查询；稀疏回放有效缓解早期任务遗忘；在协议计算资源预算下，CL-PINN总体上比固定采样和网格贪心基线提供更高且更均衡的解精度。

Conclusion: CL-PINN为在有限计算资源下学习可跨物理参数泛化的PDE解提供了一条实用路径，有潜力支撑大规模工程参数研究中的可复用物理信息代理模型。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) incorporate governing equations into neural-network training and can approximate PDE solutions without requiring large observational datasets. Parameterized PINNs (ParamPINNs) further take physical parameters as inputs, allowing a single model to represent a family of PDE solutions over a parameter domain. Existing ParamPINNs, however, still face inefficient training, uneven accuracy across parameters, and overfitting to a limited set of sampled parameter tasks, which can impair generalization to unsampled parameters. To address these issues, we propose a continual-learning physics-informed neural network (CL-PINN), which treats PDE instances at different parameter values as related tasks and learns them sequentially. CL-PINN combines Bayesian-optimization-based active parameter selection, task-wise dynamic loss weighting, sparse physics-constrained replay, and an optional parameter subnetwork to improve task allocation and knowledge retention under bounded active-task capacity. It requires no observational data and is designed to solve parameterized PDEs over relatively broad parameter domains under limited computational resources. Multi-seed evaluations on five benchmarks, including one continuous function and four parameterized PDEs, show that Bayesian selection substantially reduces objective-loss queries relative to grid-greedy search, while sparse replay mitigates forgetting of earlier tasks. Under the prescribed within-case resource protocols, CL-PINN generally provides higher and more balanced solution accuracy than fixed-sampling and grid-greedy baselines. CL-PINN offers a practical route toward learning PDE solutions that generalize across physical parameters and has the potential to support reusable physics-informed surrogates for large-scale engineering parameter studies.

</details>


### [73] [Agentic Reinforcement Learning with Observation-Calibrated Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04788)
*Yi Yang,Cong Qin,Xiaodan Liu,Chishui Chen,Qing Dong,Yan Zhang,Cao Liu,Zhao Yang,Lu Pan,Jiaye Lin,Yi Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Observation-Calibrated Self-Distillation（OCSD）方法，通过对比Full和Observation-Ablated两种结构匹配的重放视图，校准观测信息对token级监督的贡献，解决On-Policy Self-Distillation（OPSD）中重放脚手架导致的分数偏移混淆问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体常用稀疏轨迹级奖励进行强化学习训练，但该类奖励对单个token的更新强度指导有限。OPSD通过重放视图生成密集token级监督，但当未来环境观测作为特权信息时，重建扩展脚手架本身会扰动token分数，导致支持分数无法准确归因于特权信息本身，存在混淆问题。

Method: OCSD构建两个结构匹配的重放视图：Full（含实际未来观测）和Observation-Ablated（不含未来观测），两者仅在是否包含未来观测上不同。通过对比这两个视图得到观测残差，用于折扣掉重放脚手架共享的分数变化。随后将该残差用于在高不确定性步骤调节token级GRPO更新，同时保留轨迹级更新方向。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和Search-QA三个基准上，跨越三种Qwen3模型规模，OCSD一致优于强基线方法。诊断分析进一步确认校准后的残差与局部环境反馈对齐更好。

Conclusion: OCSD通过结构化对比消除重放脚手架的混淆效应，实现了更精准的token级观测信息归因，提升了LLM智能体在稀疏奖励场景下的训练效果。

Abstract: Large language model agents are commonly trained through reinforcement learning with sparse trajectory-level rewards, which offer limited guidance on how strongly individual tokens should be updated. On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) addresses this by re-scoring generated tokens under a privileged replay view to obtain dense, token-level supervision. However, we identify a confounding issue: the resulting support may reflect both the privileged information contained in the replay view and score shifts induced by the replay scaffold, making it difficult to attribute the support specifically to that information. This issue is especially pronounced when future environment observations serve as privileged information, since replaying them requires reconstructing an extended scaffold that itself perturbs token scores. To resolve this confounding, we propose Observation-Calibrated Self-Distillation (OCSD), which contrasts two structurally matched replay views, Full and Observation-Ablated, differing only in whether the actual future observation is present, to derive an observation residual that discounts score changes shared by the replay scaffold. OCSD then applies this residual to modulate token-level GRPO updates at high-uncertainty steps, while preserving the trajectory-level update direction. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and Search-QA across three Qwen3 model scales show that OCSD consistently outperforms strong baselines. Diagnostic analyses further confirm that the calibrated residual aligns better with local environment feedback. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/yiy1x/OCSD.

</details>


### [74] [Above-ground Biomass Estimation with Geospatial Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04792)
*Ghjulia Sialellia,Linus Scheibenreif,Jan Dirk Wegner,Konrad Schindler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对地球观测领域的地理空间基础模型（GFMs）进行了全球尺度的地上生物量（AGB）估算基准评测。基于AGBD数据集，作者区分两种GFT使用方式：作为冻结编码器（通过PANGAEA框架评估11个模型）和预计算嵌入产品（评估AlphaEarth Foundations和TESSERA）。结果显示冻结编码器型GFMs显著逊于监督SOTA模型，而基于AEF嵌入训练的模型反而超越监督SOTA，且在时空泛化上表现更佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确估算全球地上生物量对碳储量大尺度监测至关重要，但这是一个具有挑战性的全球尺度回归任务。虽然GFMs作为地球观测数据的通用表示学习范式已出现，但其在生物量估算等定量回归任务中的实用性尚未被充分探索，因为现有基准大多侧重分类和分割任务。因此需要系统评估GFMs在AGB估算中的表现。

Method: 使用AGBD数据集进行全球AGB估算基准测试。将GFMs分为两类评估：(i)以权重形式分发的模型，作为冻结编码器在PANGAEA基准框架内评估11个GFMs；(ii)预计算嵌入产品，评估AlphaEarth Foundations (AEF)和TESSERA。与全监督SOTA模型对比，同时评估地理和时间泛化能力，并与ESA CCI生物量产品在独立参考数据上进行一致性验证。

Result: 冻结编码器型GFMs在性能上大幅低于监督SOTA模型；而预计算嵌入产品表现出色。基于AEF嵌入训练的MLP超越了在AGBD特征上训练的监督SOTA模型；将SOTA模型在AEF嵌入（可选补充精选原始特征）上训练可获得最佳整体结果，并在空间和时间上展现出更好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: GFMs在AGB估算中的有效性高度依赖于其分发方式：预计算嵌入产品（如AEF）远优于冻结编码器的使用方式。将嵌入与监督模型结合可以超越传统的纯特征监督方法，为全球生物量估算提供了新的最佳实践方向。

Abstract: Accurate estimation of Above-Ground Biomass (AGB) from satellite imagery is essential for the large-scale monitoring of carbon stocks, yet it remains a challenging regression task at global scale. Geospatial Foundation Models (GFMs) have recently emerged as a promising machine learning paradigm to derive general-purpose representations from Earth observation data, but their utility for quantitative regression tasks like biomass estimation remains largely unexplored, as most benchmarks emphasize classification and segmentation. Here, we present a comprehensive benchmark of GFMs for global-scale AGB estimation using the AGBD dataset, a machine learning-ready benchmark spanning diverse biomes and geographies. We distinguish two ways in which GFMs reach practitioners: (i) models distributed as weights to be run by the user, which we evaluate as frozen encoders within the PANGAEA benchmarking framework; and (ii) models distributed as ready-to-use, pre-computed embedding products, for which we evaluate AlphaEarth Foundations (AEF) and TESSERA. We compare 11 GFMs available on PANGAEA and both embedding products against a fully supervised state-of-the-art (SOTA) model, assess their geographical and temporal generalization abilities, as well as agreement with the ESA CCI biomass product on independent reference data. Our results show that GFMs run as frozen encoders substantially underperform with respect to the supervised SOTA model, whereas pre-computed embedding products prove highly effective. An MLP trained on AEF embeddings outperforms the supervised SOTA model trained on AGBD features, and the same SOTA model trained on AEF embeddings (optionally augmented with selected raw features) achieves the best overall result, while also generalizing better across space and time.

</details>


### [75] [MGSB: Manifold Gated Signature Branch Pressure-Domain Baseline Architecture for Two-Phase Pipeline Flows Under Distributional Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04805)
*Issah Suleiman,Sormeh Serpoosh,Nadine Elkholy,Hicham Ferroudji,Mohammad Azizur Rahman,Matthew Hamilton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Manifold Gated Signature Bias (MGSB)，一种面向多相管道泄漏检测的流型感知架构，通过流型条件特征融合、TT-RoughPath编码器和Mean-Teacher一致性正则化，在分布偏移下显著提升检测鲁棒性，在leave-one-group-out评估中达到0.930的检测F1和0.783的OOD F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多相管道的泄漏检测模型在部署时，若遇到与训练时不同的流型（如从气泡流到段塞流），性能会显著退化。现有评估方法通常在分布内条件下进行，掩盖了流型转换导致的失败问题。因此需要一种对流型变化具有鲁棒性的检测架构。

Method: MGSB包含三个核心组件：(1)流型条件特征融合（regime-conditioned feature fusion）进行流型感知的特征整合；(2)TT-RoughPath编码器用于捕捉路径签名特征；(3)Mean-Teacher一致性正则化以增强模型在分布偏移下的稳定性。通过leave-one-group-out评估验证模型的OOD泛化能力。

Result: MGSB在leave-one-group-out评估中取得检测F1为0.930、OOD F1为0.783，显著优于CNN-LSTM和全连接基线在严重特征损坏下的表现。消融实验证明架构设计而非训练流程是OOD鲁棒性的主要贡献因素；Mahalanobis距离分析确认held-out条件确实属于分布外数据。

Conclusion: 显式的流型感知建模是提升工业多相管道泄漏检测鲁棒性、实现传感器无关检测的可行路径。

Abstract: Leak detection models for multiphase pipelines often degrade when deployed under flow regimes that differ from training. Existing evaluations typically assess performance under in-distribution operating conditions, masking failures caused by regime transitions such as bubble-to-slug flow. We propose the Manifold Gated Signature Bias (MGSB), a regime-aware architecture combining regime-conditioned feature fusion, a TT-RoughPath encoder, and Mean-Teacher consistency regularization to improve robustness under distribution shift. Under leave-one-group-out evaluation, MGSB achieves a detection F1 of 0.930 and an OOD F1 of 0.783, substantially outperforming CNN-LSTM and fully connected baselines under severe feature corruption. Ablations show the proposed architecture, not the training procedure, is the primary contributor to OOD robustness, while Mahalanobis-distance analysis confirms the held-out conditions are genuinely out-of-distribution. These results show that explicit regime-aware modelling is a practical path toward robust, sensor-agnostic leak detection in industrial multiphase pipelines.

</details>


### [76] [Consistency-Driven Co-Evolution for Self-Supervised Cross-Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04926)
*Xuehang Guo,Pengyuan Li,Tom Hope,Tirthankar Ghosal,Manling Li,Qingyun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoCoEvolve框架，通过定义图表、表格、代码三种表示之间的显式一一对应关系，并利用表示间的一致性作为优化信号（无需额外标注），实现了跨表示理解的一致性优化，在训练时（CoCoEvolve@Train）和测试时（CoCoEvolve@Test）均能提升性能，并提供了覆盖六种跨表示任务的评估套件CoCoEvolve@Eval。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图表图像、表格数据和可视化代码在多个领域日益重要，但跨表示理解面临三大挑战：表示之间的关系本质上是"一对多"的，人工监督模糊且昂贵，且缺乏一个既方向自适应又能跨表示泛化的原理性优化信号。现有的任务特定目标无法解决这些根本性问题。

Method: CoCoEvolve的核心思想是将原本一对多的跨表示映射问题转化为显式的一对一对应关系，并以表示之间的"一致性"作为优化目标，无需额外标注。具体包含三个组件：CoCoEvolve@Train在训练阶段沿图表-表格-代码闭环进行协同演化优化；CoCoEvolve@Test在推理阶段应用相同的一致性目标进行测试时协同优化；CoCoEvolve@Eval提供覆盖全部六种跨表示任务的评估套件。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上，CoCoEvolve无论是训练时设置还是测试时设置均提升了模型性能，验证了该一致性优化框架的有效性。

Conclusion: CoCoEvolve通过利用表示间一致性这一自监督信号，绕过了一对多映射的模糊性问题，提供了一种无需额外标注、方向自适应且跨表示通用的优化框架，有效提升了图表、表格、代码三种表示之间的跨理解一致性。

Abstract: As chart images, tabular data, and visualization code play increasingly important roles across diverse domains, cross-representation understanding across these modalities poses fundamental challenges for AI systems: the relationships across representations are inherently \textit{one-to-many}, supervision is ambiguous and costly, and model optimization lacks a principled signal that is both direction-adaptive and representation-generalizable beyond task-specific objectives. We introduce CoCoEvolve to improve consistency across chart, table, and code representations. Instead of treating cross-representation mapping as a one-to-many problem, we define explicit one-to-one correspondences and optimize models using agreement between representations, without additional annotations. During training, CoCoEvolve@Train performs co-evolution across the chart-table-code cycle, while CoCoEvolve@Test applies the same consistency objective at inference time for test-time co-optimization. We also present CoCoEvolve@Eval, an evaluation suite covering all six cross-representation tasks. Across four benchmarks, CoCoEvolve improves performance in both training-time and test-time settings. Our project page: https://xhguo7.github.io/CoCoEvolve/.

</details>


### [77] [Robust Control under Stationary Ambiguity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04832)
*Konrad J. Mueller,Amira Akkari,Ben Wood,Lukas Gonon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"平稳模糊性"(stationary ambiguity)概念，用于训练在仿真环境中对潜在参数变化保持持续鲁棒性的控制策略。不同于传统方法中参数不确定性随时间推断而消退，该方法通过构造潜在状态滤波过程保持平稳的仿真器，使策略在面对金融市场价格波动等情境下始终维持鲁棒性。实验表明该方法在期权对冲问题中表现优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在仿真中优化的控制策略应用到真实系统时，由于仿真器参数仅从有限数据估计且未在仿真中体现参数不确定性，策略会表现不佳。传统做法是在仿真中对每条轨迹随机采样参数以引入模糊性，但随着策略逐步观察系统，它会逐渐推断出参数值从而丧失鲁棒性。在金融市场中，潜在因素（如波动率制度）预期会变化，这种鲁棒性丧失是不可取的，因此需要让策略持续保持鲁棒性。

Method: 提出"平稳模糊性"原则：要求仿真器在潜在状态上诱导一个平稳的滤波过程，使模糊性随系统状态变化但不会随时间系统性衰减。具体包括：构造满足平稳性的仿真器；选择能产生真实仿真的模型；随机化参数的方法；以及仿真器和策略的初始化方式。论文以衍生品对冲问题为实验场景验证该方法。

Result: 在对冲问题上，训练于平稳模糊性环境下的策略相比传统方法对潜在因素保持持久鲁棒性，在真实市场数据上表现优异。

Conclusion: 平稳模糊性是一种有价值的建模原则，适用于对冲及其他由外生随机过程驱动、具备转移潜在结构的序列控制问题。它指导了仿真器的模型选择、参数随机化方式以及初始化等多个设计决策。

Abstract: Control policies optimized in simulation can perform poorly in the real system when the parameters $x$ of the simulator are estimated from limited data but the resulting parameter uncertainty is not represented inside the simulation. A common way to incorporate such ambiguity is to simulate each trajectory of the system under a randomly drawn value for $x$. Since the policy cannot observe the drawn value, it must initially choose controls that perform well across many possible parameter values. However, if the policy progressively observes the system, it can often gradually infer the value of $x$, so that ambiguity vanishes. Over time, the policy then specializes to its estimate of $x$ and loses its robustness. This is undesirable in many real systems, where latent factors are expected to shift. In financial markets, for example, a policy hedging a derivative payoff should remain robust to changes in the volatility regime. To induce such continual robustness, we propose training policies in simulators where ambiguity varies with the system's state but does not systematically decay over time. We formalize this requirement as stationary ambiguity: the simulator should induce a stationary filter process over the latent state. We show how to construct such simulators and demonstrate, on hedging problems, that policies trained under stationary ambiguity preserve robustness to latent factors over time, leading to strong performance on real market data. As a modeling principle, stationary ambiguity informs many simulator design decisions: which models make realistic simulators, how their parameters should be randomized, and how simulator and policy should be initialized. While our experiments focus on hedging, stationary ambiguity may also be useful for other sequential control problems driven by exogenous stochastic processes with shifting latent structure.

</details>


### [78] [SVI-DAG: A Structured Variational Inference Approach to Bayesian Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04930)
*Shrenik Zinage*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SVI-DAG是一种结构化变分推断方法，用于贝叶斯因果发现，利用归一化流建模边间的依赖关系，并通过斯坦因变分梯度下降在无环性空间中更新节点势能，以支持表达性强且多模态的DAG后验学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的贝叶斯因果发现方法存在两个关键局限：一是无法编码边之间的依赖关系，二是缺乏以原则性方式将领域知识作为归纳偏置纳入搜索过程的机制。此外，在观测数据有限和可识别性问题下，精确近似DAG后验分布非常困难。

Method: 提出SVI-DAG框架，采用结构化变分推断进行贝叶斯因果发现。核心组件包括：(1) 使用归一化流来建模边之间的依赖关系，支持表达性强且多模态的DAG后验学习；(2) 使用斯坦因变分梯度下降（SVGD）结合无环性空间中的核函数来更新节点势能，以缓解ELBO优化中的模式寻求行为并促进模式覆盖；同时支持将先验信念作为归纳偏置纳入学习过程。

Result: 在与5种最先进的贝叶斯DAG学习方法对比中，SVI-DAG在不确定性量化方面表现出优越性能，同时在结构准确性方面保持竞争力。

Conclusion: SVI-DAG通过结构化变分推断与归一化流和SVGD的结合，有效解决了现有贝叶斯DAG学习方法在边依赖建模和先验知识融入方面的不足，在不确定性量化上达到最优性能并保持结构准确性。

Abstract: Bayesian causal discovery seeks to determine the posterior distribution of causal theories, which are interpreted as directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) that explain the observed data. The resulting posterior allows systematic reasoning regarding epistemic uncertainty within these theories. Nonetheless, finding such graphs is difficult due to identifiability problems and limited observational data. Furthermore, precisely approximating posterior over graphs is challenging given vast range of potential DAGs. Recent Bayesian approaches have addressed some of these challenges, yet they remain limited as they fail to encode dependencies between edges, and lack principled ways to incorporate domain knowledge as inductive biases during the search process. To overcome these limitations, we propose SVI-DAG, a structured variational inference approach to Bayesian causal discovery using observational data and prior beliefs that uses normalizing flows to model dependencies between edges, supporting expressive and multimodal posterior learning over DAGs. To mitigate mode seeking behaviour in evidence lower bound optimization and promote mode coverage, we use stein variational gradient descent to update the node potentials using a kernel in acyclicity space. We evaluate SVI-DAG against 5 state-of-the-art Bayesian DAG learning methods and demonstrate superior performance in uncertainty quantification while remaining competitive in terms of structural accuracy.

</details>


### [79] [Training Crossroads for Recurrent Vision Transformers: Recurrence, Neural ODEs, and Deep Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04879)
*Grzegorz Gruszczynski,Pawel Olszowiec,Michal Byra,Grzegorz Stefanski,Alberto Presta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对单块循环Vision Transformer（bViT）在CIFAR-100协议下进行了受控的经验性表征研究，考察了三种训练与推理机制：循环与独立参数化深度的比较（在匹配FLOPs或内存约束下）、ODE求解器阶数的作用（是数值细化还是架构偏置）、以及训练视界之外的鲁棒性代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准ViT的参数数量随深度线性增长，而单块循环ViT（bViT）通过重复共享单一块来消除这一增长。作者并非提出新架构，而是希望深入理解固定bViT在不同训练/推理机制下的行为特性，回答循环是否优于独立参数化深度、ODE求解器阶数的本质作用、以及循环在训练视界之外的鲁棒性问题。

Method: 在统一的CIFAR-100协议下，对固定的bViT模型进行受控经验性实验。研究了三种机制：(i) 在匹配FLOPs或匹配参数内存约束下比较循环深度与独立参数化深度；(ii) 使用ODE求解器训练残差循环块，考察求解器阶数的影响；(iii) 引入阶段式深度监督以探索精确度-鲁棒性前沿。

Result: 研究表明：在FLOPs为主要约束时标准ViT更优；在内存约束下循环ViT提供更好的准确率-参数权衡。连续时间类比下残差循环块的真实向量场为ż=Fθ(z)-z；高阶求解器作为求解器诱导的架构偏置而非数值精度改进，且增益不均匀。阶段式深度监督不提升名义准确率，但使模型在远超训练视界时优雅退化，而朴素循环则坍缩至接近随机性能。

Conclusion: 循环ViT的价值取决于约束条件：在内存受限场景下优于标准ViT，但无法替代FLOPs敏感场景中的标准架构。残差循环块的ODE连续化应使用状态相减的向量场表达，高阶求解器的作用应被理解为架构偏置而非数值细化。阶段式深度监督为实现循环模型的鲁棒性外推提供了有效手段。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) achieve strong image-recognition performance, but their parameter count grows linearly with depth when each block is independently parameterized. Single-block recurrent ViTs (bViT) remove this growth by repeatedly applying one shared block. Rather than proposing a new architecture, we fix a bViT and provide a controlled empirical characterization of three training and inference regimes under a common CIFAR-100 protocol, asking: (i)~when does recurrence beat independently parameterized depth---at matched FLOPs or at matched parameter memory? (ii)~when a residual recurrent block is trained through an ODE solver, does solver order act as numerical refinement or as an architectural bias? and (iii)~what does robustness beyond the training horizon cost in nominal accuracy? We find that standard ViTs remain preferable when FLOPs are the primary constraint, whereas recurrent ViTs offer a better accuracy--parameter trade-off under memory constraints. Consistent with the standard view of residual networks as Euler discretizations of ODEs, the continuous-time analogue of a residual recurrent block is the state-subtracted vector field $\dot{z}=F_θ(z)-z$; although known in principle, this distinction is easy to violate when the block is wrapped as a black-box vector field, and we qualify the cost at few accuracy points. Because the vector field is learned jointly with the solver, higher-order solvers act as a solver-induced architectural bias rather than a numerical-accuracy improvement, and their gains are not uniform. Finally, stage-wise deep supervision traces an accuracy--robustness frontier: it does not improve nominal accuracy, but degrades gracefully far beyond the training horizon, where naive recurrence collapses to near-random performance.

</details>


### [80] [CheMLFlow: An Open-Source Platform for Cheminformatics and Materials Informatics Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04942)
*Brendan Smith,Susana Lopez-Moreno,Eric Dolores-Cuenca,Sangil Kim,Jose L. Mendoza-Cortes,Nijamudheen Abdulrahiman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CheMLFlow是一个开源平台，用于构建端到端、高通量和智能体的科学机器学习工作流，提供模块化组件、参考流水线、标准化工件和评估输出，支持分子化学及其他领域的可复现基准测试，并在量子力学、物理化学和生物活性性质预测上达到文献级性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科研机器学习开发中存在常见瓶颈：研究人员即使只关注一个阶段（如模型训练），也常需自行组装数据采集、标注、表示、训练、验证、筛选、解释和报告等多个步骤为可复现的流水线，编排开销高且缺乏标准化，阻碍了方法间的系统比较和基准测试。

Method: CheMLFlow提供模块化工作流组件、即用参考流水线、标准化工件和评估输出；支持可插拔的表示与模型、确定性数据划分、显式运行工件、批量执行和报告生成。其配置驱动的工作流和结构化输出为编码代理提供了实用接口，使AI代理能在人类监督下辅助构建实验、检查结果和总结发现。

Result: 平台在量子力学（QM）、物理化学和生物活性性质预测等基准上达到文献水平性能，并通过时间序列数据集展示了超越分子化学数据集的更广泛应用场景。

Conclusion: CheMLFlow通过降低编排开销和提供标准化基准基础设施，解决了科研ML流水线复现性不足的问题，同时为未来智能体辅助科学实验提供了友好的自动化接口，是科学软件向智能体辅助实验方向演进的重要实践。

Abstract: CheMLFlow is an open-source platform for building and executing end-to-end, high-throughput, and agentic workflows for scientific and technological applications. CheMLFlow targets a common bottleneck in scientific machine learning development, where researchers often need to assemble data acquisition, curation, representation, model training, validation, screening, interpretation, and reporting into a reproducible pipeline, even when their primary research contribution concerns only one stage. CheMLFlow provides modular workflow components, ready-to-run reference pipelines, standardized artifacts, and evaluation outputs that reduce orchestration overhead and support benchmarking across methods and datasets. The platform is designed to be extensible, reproducible, and automation friendly, with pluggable representations and models, deterministic splits, explicit run artifacts, batch execution, and report generation. As scientific software increasingly moves toward agent assisted experimentation, CheMLFlow's configuration driven workflows and structured outputs also provide a practical interface for coding agents to help users construct experiments, inspect results, and summarize findings under human supervision. This article describes the system architecture, core workflows, and benchmarks that reach literature performance for quantum mechanical, physicochemical and bioactivity property prediction, and use cases involving time series datasets demonstrating applications beyond molecular chemistry datasets.

</details>


### [81] [Variational Bounds for Perceptron Learning from Structured Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04882)
*Francesco Camilli,Pierluigi Contucci,Federica Gerace,Emanuele Mingione*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对高斯混合训练下的有限温度连续自旋感知机的变分方法，通过插值法与对数凹性及集中不等式相结合，推导出极限淬火压力的上下极小极大变分界，并在两个变分参数优化可交换时得到精确解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为有限温度连续自旋感知机在高斯混合数据上的训练问题建立严格的变分框架，克服已有方法在非凸优化和广义先验/效用函数下难以获得精确解的困难。

Method: 结合插值方法（interpolation method）与对数凹性和浓度估计，推导下限与上限的两个极小极大变分界；两个界仅在两个变分参数的优化顺序上不同，其余极值受变分势的凹-凸结构控制。

Result: 当两个变分参数的优化可交换时，上下界匹配并唯一确定模型解；同一变分势同时给出固定点方程（作为平稳性条件），并为基态能量、训练损失和泛化误差的计算提供统一路径。

Conclusion: 该方法适用于广泛的凹效用函数和对数凹可分离先验测度，提供了一个统一的解析框架来计算该感知机模型的多个核心量（能量、损失、泛化误差），并在优化可交换情形下实现精确解。

Abstract: We introduce a variational approach to a finite-temperature continuous-spin perceptron trained on a Gaussian mixture. The model allows for a broad class of concave utilities and log-concave separable prior measures on the spins. By combining the interpolation method with log-concavity and concentration estimates, we derive lower and upper minimax variational bounds for the limiting quenched pressure. Remarkably, the two bounds differ only in the order of optimization of two variational parameters, while all remaining extrema are controlled by the concave--convex structure of the variational potential. Whenever the two optimizations commute, the two bounds match and identify the solution of the model. The same potential yields the fixed-point equations as stationarity conditions and provides a unified route to the computation of the ground-state energy, training loss, and generalization error.

</details>


### [82] [MultiPathFormer: Towards a Foundation Model for Multipath Wireless Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05076)
*Blessed Guda,Kayley Sze,Carlee Joe-Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MultiPathFormer，一种以多径传播路径为预训练对象的无线自回归基础模型，通过下一路径预测实现预训练，并引入环境RAG机制和首路径码本，在路径统计估计、下游任务（定位、波束预测、视距分类、信道估计）中均优于现有基于信道张量的基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无线基础模型基于信道张量进行掩码重建预训练，忽视了无线传播的物理特性（多径传播），导致预训练表征缺乏物理可解释性和泛化能力。作者认为应以多径传播作为更本质的预训练对象，以学习可复用的无线传播表征。

Method: MultiPathFormer将每个发射-接收链路表示为连续值路径令牌的有序序列，使用自回归方式通过下一路径预测（next-path prediction）进行预训练。在transformer骨干基础上引入两个关键组件：环境RAG（检索增强生成）机制，利用环境知识改进路径统计估计；以及首路径码本（first-path codebook），用于建模首达路径的先验信息。

Result: 在27个环境上预训练后，路径统计估计（如时延和功率）提升最高达59%。模型可迁移到未见用户；经过场景特定微调后，在新环境中优于从零训练的模型。在下游任务中，定位误差5.57m，top-3波束准确率0.914，视距分类准确率0.994，信道估计NMSE 0.561，均优于现有SOTA基于信道的无线基础模型。

Conclusion: 路径级预训练能够学习可复用的无线传播表征，相比信道级预训练更具物理意义和泛化能力，为无线基础模型的设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in machine learning have enabled training of wireless foundation models, which aim to support tasks such as channel estimation, beam prediction, and localization based on wireless signals. Existing wireless foundation models typically pretrain on channel tensors using masked reconstruction over subcarriers, antennas, or time but ignore the physical characteristics of wireless propagation. In this work, we propose to instead use multipath propagation as the fundamental pretraining object. We present MultiPathFormer, an autoregressive foundation model that represents each transmitter-receiver link as an ordered sequence of continuous-valued path tokens and pretrains with next-path prediction. We introduce an Environmental RAG (retrieval-augmented generation) mechanism and a first-path codebook on top of the transformer backbone, leveraging environment knowledge to improve path statistics estimation like delay and power by up to 59%. MultiPathFormer pretrained on 27 environments transfers to unseen users and, after scenario-specific fine-tuning, outperforms training the corresponding models from scratch in new environments. Across downstream tasks, it outperforms SOTA channel-based foundation models, achieving 5.57 m mean localization error, 0.914 top-3 beam accuracy, 0.994 line-of-sight classification accuracy, and 0.561 channel estimation NMSE. These results show that path-level pretraining can learn reusable representations of wireless propagation.

</details>


### [83] [Capability-Gated Planning: Cost-to-Goal Discovery and the Limits of Myopic Experiment Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05085)
*Ahmed Hassoon,Mark Dredze*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文识别了自动化科学发现系统中一个结构性局限：当决策基于短视的得分函数（如每单位成本的信息增益）时，无法给"建设性行动"（即获取实验能力/工具等）赋予价值，因为这些行动在短视视界内不产生信息。作者证明了所有短视信息最大化规划器在最坏情况下具有无界近似比甚至无法到达目标，并提出了一种能力感知的增量重规划器CG-Plan来克服这一局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化科学发现系统常用短视的评分函数（如期望信息增益/成本）来决定下一步行动，但这无法处理需要先构建能力（如仪器、化验、管线、模拟器、抽象）才能获得答案的任务。建设性行动在有限视界内不产生即时信息，因此短视规划器会忽略它，导致无法完成需要能力链的任务。

Method: 将目标导向的发现过程形式化为信念空间中的随机最短路径问题，其中建设性实验改变下游动作图。证明了能力不可区分引理：在视界内，获取能力与执行空操作在观测上不可区分。基于此提出CG-Plan，一个增量重规划器，使用能力感知的cost-to-go启发式h = h_cap + h_exp（能力分量加经验分量）。

Result: 证明对任意视界深度d，存在实例使得所有短视信息最大化规划器的近似比无界，且存在相关实例使其永远无法到达目标。指出能力门控是独立于曲率（次模性）和信息顺序（自适应差距）的困难维度。在受控测试平台上，性能差距仅在门控条件下出现，对所有固定视界持续存在，且当近失假设来自数据一致的提议者时产生。

Conclusion: 短视规划在自动化科学发现中存在系统性盲区——无法评估建设性行动的价值。CG-Plan通过能力感知的规划启发式弥补了这一缺陷，实现了对能力门控场景的有效处理。

Abstract: Systems that automate scientific discovery must repeatedly decide which experiment to run, which hypothesis to test, which tool to build, and when to stop. Many systems make these decisions by maximizing a myopic score such as expected information gain per unit cost or a learned plausibility score. We identify a structural limitation of this approach. Some actions are constructive: they acquire an epistemic capability (an instrument, assay, pipeline, simulator, or abstraction) whose value lies not in the information returned immediately but in the future actions it makes available. When the least-cost route to a confident answer requires a chain of such constructions, a planner that scores actions only by information obtainable within a bounded horizon cannot value the first construction: it yields no information within the horizon and is dominated by any measurement with positive information, however small. We formulate goal-directed discovery as a stochastic shortest-path problem in belief space in which constructive experiments change the downstream action graph, and prove that for every lookahead depth d there is an instance on which every myopic information-maximizing planner has an unbounded approximation ratio, and a related instance on which it never reaches the goal. The mechanism is a capability-indistinguishability lemma: within the horizon, acquiring a capability can be observationally indistinguishable from paying for a null action. This establishes capability gating as a reachability axis of difficulty distinct from curvature (submodularity) and information order (adaptivity gaps). We introduce CG-Plan, an incremental replanner with a capability-aware cost-to-go heuristic h = h_cap + h_exp. In a controlled testbed, the performance gap appears only under gating, persists for every fixed horizon, and arises when near-miss hypotheses come from a data-consistent proposer.

</details>


### [84] [Optimal Training-Time Scaling in Gradual Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04927)
*Zonghuan Xu,Krishna Harish*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究渐进适应中训练时间随中间任务数量变化的最优策略，发现每个任务的最优训练时间与任务总数成反比（s_N^*=Θ(N^{-1})），即总训练时间固定时效果最佳。基于过参数化线性回归的连续极限分析，证明训练时间过短或过长都会导致学习进展减小，并在MNIST旋转和Yearbook数据集上验证了理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 渐进适应（gradual adaptation）中，当中间任务数量增加时，每个任务上的训练时间应如何调整是一个尚未明确回答的问题。理解最优训练时间与任务数量之间的缩放关系，对于设计高效的多任务/连续学习算法至关重要。

Method: 1. 在过参数化线性回归任务上建模，假设任务平滑变化且共享零损失解。2. 通过令Ns_N→τ建立连续极限框架。3. 推导出极限学习进展对τ的标度关系：小τ时progress=Θ(τ)，大τ时progress=Θ(τ^{-1})。4. 据此求解最优每任务训练时间。5. 在逐渐旋转的MNIST和Yearbook时间偏移数据上进行实验验证。

Result: 1. 极限学习进展在τ很小时为Θ(τ)，在τ很大时为Θ(τ^{-1})，呈现非单调的先增后减。2. 最优每任务训练时间s_N^*=Θ(N^{-1})，即总训练时间Ns_N^*=Θ(1)为常数。3. 在MNIST旋转和Yearbook实验中，与理论一致：路径划分越细，每任务训练时间越少。

Conclusion: 在渐进适应的连续极限下，训练时间过短或过长均不利于最终学习进展，最优策略是在任务总数增加时按比例减少每个任务的训练时间，保持总训练时间恒定。这一结论在理论和实验上均得到了验证。

Abstract: In gradual adaptation, how should the training time on each task change as the number of intermediate tasks increases? We study this question for overparameterized linear regression tasks that change smoothly and share a zero-loss solution. With $N$ tasks and training time $s_N$ on each, the final learning progress converges to a continuum curve when $Ns_N\toτ$. The limiting progress is $Θ(τ)$ for small $τ$ and $Θ(τ^{-1})$ for large $τ$, so both very short and very long training produce little progress. It follows that optimal per-task training times scale as $s_N^\star=Θ(N^{-1})$, equivalently $Ns_N^\star=Θ(1)$. Experiments on gradually rotated MNIST and a natural Yearbook time shift are consistent with less per-task training as the path is divided more finely.

</details>


### [85] [SSTQ:Privacy-Preserving Vector Quantization via Subsampled Stochastic TurboQuant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05127)
*Adel Javanmard,David P. Woodruff,Vahab Mirrokni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SSTQ（Subsampled Stochastic TurboQuant）框架，结合过完备等范数紧框架、坐标子采样和隐私感知的一维量化，在分布式优化中同时实现局部差分隐私与低通信开销。SSTQ 包含 Flat Randomized Response 和 Metric-Aware Laplace 两个变体，在 CIFAR-10 和 Fashion-MNIST 联邦学习任务上展现出优于现有基线的效用与通信效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式优化中实现局部差分隐私（LDP）并保持低通信成本仍具挑战性。现有的向量量化方法（如 vqSGD）依赖高维几何构造，但会产生不利的维度相关方差，影响隐私与效用之间的平衡。

Method: SSTQ 框架将三个关键组件集成在一起：(1) 过完备等范数紧帧（overcomplete equal-norm tight frames），用于高效编码；(2) 坐标子采样，降低维度影响；(3) 隐私感知的一维量化，提供隐私保障。框架包含两个变体：Flat Randomized Response 版本和 Metric-Aware Laplace 版本，后者更适合更高的码本位宽场景。此外，作者推导了隐私感知的码本目标函数，将码本相关的 MSE 缩放从 O(4^b) 降至 O(2^b)。

Result: SSTQ 在仅使用 ⌈log₂N⌉ + b 比特/客户端的情况下（N=Θ(d) 为帧大小）实现了最优的均方误差（MSE）缩放。推导的隐私感知码本目标将码本相关 MSE 从 O(4^b) 降至 O(2^b)。在 CIFAR-10 和 Fashion-MNIST 联邦学习任务上的实验表明，SSTQ 在效用和通信效率方面优于既有基线方法。

Conclusion: SSTQ 通过将几何构造、子采样与隐私感知量化相结合，成功解决了分布式优化中局部差分隐私与通信效率之间的权衡问题，在理论最优性和实际效果之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Achieving local differential privacy in distributed optimization while maintaining low communication cost remains challenging. Existing vector quantization methods, such as vqSGD, use high-dimensional geometric constructions but incur unfavorable dimension-dependent variance. In this work, we propose Subsampled Stochastic TurboQuant (SSTQ), a framework that combines overcomplete equal-norm tight frames, coordinate subsampling, and privacy-aware one-dimensional quantization. SSTQ includes two variants: a Flat Randomized Response version and a Metric-Aware Laplace version, the latter being better suited to higher codebook bit-width regimes. We show that SSTQ achieves optimal mean squared error scaling while using only $\lceil \log_2 N \rceil + b$ bits per client, where $N = Θ(d)$ is the frame size. We also derive a surrogate privacy-aware codebook objective that reduces the codebook-dependent MSE scaling from $O(4^b)$ to $O(2^b)$. Finally, we empirically evaluate SSTQ against established baselines on federated learning tasks using CIFAR-10 and Fashion-MNIST, demonstrating favorable utility and communication efficiency.

</details>


### [86] [SpecRoll: Fast-Slow Verifier-Feedback Adaptation for Speculative Reinforcement Learning Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04962)
*Nhat Minh Pham,Duy Tung Doan,Thi Duyen Ngo,Vinh Van Nguyen,Khac-Hoai Nam Bui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SpecRoll是一种面向强化学习（RL）后训练阶段的投机式rollout生成引擎，通过双时间尺度适应机制（快速future-token heads和慢速Reflex模块）在不改变目标策略采样分布及GRPO目标的前提下，实现1.26-2.15x生成加速和1.21-2.04x端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL后训练能显著提升大语言模型的推理能力，但自回归rollout生成是主要的效率瓶颈。投机解码虽可加速生成，但直接应用于RL时面临挑战：目标策略持续演化，静态proposer会过时，而频繁更新drafter则带来巨大额外开销。

Method: SpecRoll包含三个核心组件：1）轻量级future-token heads并行生成提议；2）Reflex模块利用延迟的验证器反馈进行有界、轨迹局部的隐状态修正（无需反向传播）；3）慢路径仅在检测到持续性能下降时才更新head参数。同时结合并发送感知的稀疏树验证和精确目标验证，保持目标rollout分布和GRPO目标不变。

Result: 在五个模型（1.5B到14B）和三个数学推理数据集上，SpecRoll相对vanilla GRPO取得1.26-2.15x生成加速和1.21-2.04x端到端加速；在全部15个匹配设置中均优于FastGRPO，平均端到端增益1.18x。消融实验表明快慢两条适应路径具有互补性。

Conclusion: SpecRoll通过双时间尺度适应机制有效解决了RL中投机解码由于策略演化导致的proposer过时问题，在保持目标分布和RL目标不变的前提下显著提升训练效率，为大规模RL后训练提供了实用的加速方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training improves the reasoning capabilities of large language models, but autoregressive rollout generation remains a major efficiency bottleneck. Speculative decoding can accelerate generation, yet applying it during RL is difficult because the target policy continually evolves: static proposers become stale, while frequent drafter updates add substantial overhead. We introduce SpecRoll, a speculative rollout engine that preserves the target model's sampling distribution while adapting at two timescales. Lightweight future-token heads generate parallel proposals, while our proposed Reflex module uses delayed verifier feedback to perform bounded, trajectory-local hidden-state corrections without backpropagation. A complementary slow path updates the head parameters only when sustained degradation is detected. SpecRoll combines these mechanisms with concurrency-aware sparse-tree verification and exact target verification, leaving the target rollout distribution and GRPO objective unchanged. Across five models ranging from 1.5B to 14B and three mathematical reasoning datasets, SpecRoll achieves 1.26-2.15x generation speedup and 1.21-2.04x end-to-end speedup over vanilla GRPO. It also outperforms FastGRPO in both generation and end-to-end time across all 15 matched settings, with an average pairwise end-to-end gain of 1.18x. Controlled ablations show that the fast and slow adaptation paths provide complementary benefits. Our source code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SpecRoll-26062006.

</details>


### [87] [EvolveNet: Collaborative Harness Evolution for Agent Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04968)
*Jun Nie,Yonggang Zhang,Qianshu Cai,Yiu-ming Cheung,Xinmei Tian,Bo Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EvolveNet提出了一种协作式harness进化范式，将经验提取从中央优化器转移到数据端，各数据本地agent在自身负载上进化共享harness，仅将程序适应结果聚合回共享版本并重新分发，从而实现无需共享原始数据即可让所有参与者继承彼此的运行经验。为此引入作用域类型化和证据引导的程序聚合机制解决程序组合冲突，在五个基准场景中均取得改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM agent harness进化方法假设所有执行经验可以路由到单一优化器，沿一条顺序轨迹进化一个harness。但真实agent生态系统中，用户、组织和环境产生的经验流是隔离的、无法汇集的，而最有学习价值的恰恰是无法集中化的数据。因此需要一种能够在不共享原始负载的情况下协作进化harness的范式。

Method: EvolveNet将经验提取下沉到数据端：共享harness被广播到各数据本地agent部署，每个部署在自身工作负载上独立进化harness；仅将产生的程序适应结果组合成更新后的共享harness并重新分发。针对独立修改的程序无法像模型参数那样求平均且组合时可能冲突的问题，引入作用域类型化（scope-typed）和证据引导（evidence-guided）的程序聚合机制。

Result: 在五个设置中（text-to-SQL、数据科学编程、竞赛编程、软件工程和agentic工作流），EvolveNet在所有五个场景均改进了共享harness，在异构工作负载下增益最大。消融实验表明改进源于不同agent（原创）适应的组合而非简单的选择。

Conclusion: EvolveNet证明将聚合边界从原始工作负载转移到学习到的适应上，既保持了工作负载的本地性，又允许多个进化搜索并发进行并降低串行深度，为真实异构agent生态系统中协作式harness进化提供了一种有效的范式和机制。

Abstract: The capabilities of an LLM agent depend not only on its model but on the harness: the executable program that constructs context, invokes tools, verifies results, and recovers from failure. Recent work shows that evolving the harness yields persistent improvements without updating model weights. Existing approaches, however, assume that all execution experience can be routed to a single optimizer, which evolves one harness along a sequential trajectory. Real agent ecosystems violate that assumption: users, organizations, and environments generate isolated streams of experience that cannot be pooled, so the experience most worth learning from is exactly the experience that cannot be directly centralized. We introduce EvolveNet, a paradigm of collaborative harness evolution that moves experience extraction to the data. A shared harness is broadcast to data-local agent deployments, each of which evolves it on its own workload. Only the resulting program adaptations are composed into an updated shared harness and redistributed, so that every participating agent inherits operational experience discovered by the others. By shifting the aggregation boundary from raw workloads to learned adaptations, EvolveNet keeps workloads local and allows multiple evolutionary searches to proceed concurrently with reduced serial depth. Because independently modified programs cannot be averaged like model parameters and may conflict when composed, EvolveNet introduces scope-typed, evidence-guided program aggregation. Across five settings spanning text-to-SQL, data-science coding, competitive programming, software engineering, and agentic workflows, EvolveNet improves the shared harness in all five, with the largest gains under heterogeneous workloads, and ablations attribute the improvement to composition of adaptations from different agents rather than to selecting among them.

</details>


### [88] [Stochastic Emulation using Generalized Stratified Sampling for Performance-Based Risk Optimization of Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05006)
*Isabela D. Rodrigues,Seymour M. J. Spence,Henrique M. Kroetz,André T. Beck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将广义分层采样（GSS）与随机多项式混沌展开（SPCE）相结合的GSS-SPCE框架，用于性能基准风险优化（PBRO）中结构在随机荷载作用下的可靠性与优化问题。该框架通过按灾害强度对输入空间分层并在每层内训练独立SPCE代理模型，改善了极端响应尾部区域的表征精度，并利用全概率定理重组各层条件超越概率以评估概率约束。在两层钢框架建筑屈曲约束支撑截面优化设计中验证了其准确性和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SPCE作为一种无需在固定输入条件下复制非线性分析的随机仿真器，虽然比传统元模型更具优势，但在准确表征结构响应分布尾部（极端响应）方面存在局限性，这直接影响PBRO中概率约束评估的精度。

Method: 提出GSS-SPCE框架：首先利用GSS方案根据灾害强度将输入空间划分为多个层；然后在每一层内独立训练SPCE仿真器以表征该层内的响应分布；最后通过全概率定理将各层估计的条件超越概率重组，用于评估整体概率约束。将该框架应用于两层钢框架建筑中屈曲约束支撑截面面积的最优设计。

Result: 结果表明，所提出的GSS-SPCE框架能够准确估计结构响应分布（包括尾部区域），同时大幅减少PBRO所需的非线性模型评估次数，在精度与计算效率之间取得了良好平衡。

Conclusion: GSS-SPCE框架有效克服了SPCE在极端响应尾部表征上的不足，为性能基准风险优化提供了一种兼具准确性和计算效率的可靠方案，适用于随机荷载作用下结构的最优设计问题。

Abstract: Metamodels are instrumental in reducing the computational burden associated with nested reliability analyses and optimization loops in Performance-Based Risk Optimization (PBRO) of structures under stochastic loads. In this context, stochastic emulators are particularly useful because they approximate response distributions while accounting for the intrinsic stochasticity of the simulator. Among these methods, Stochastic Polynomial Chaos Expansion (SPCE) is especially attractive because it does not require replications of nonlinear analyses at fixed input conditions. However, SPCE may present limitations in accurately representing extreme responses in the tails of structural response distributions. To address this limitation, this study proposes a framework that combines Generalized Stratified Sampling (GSS) with SPCE. The GSS scheme partitions the input space into strata according to the intensity of the hazard, improving the representation of extreme responses, while independent SPCE emulators are trained within each stratum. The conditional exceedance probabilities estimated in each stratum are then recombined using the total probability theorem to evaluate the probabilistic constraints. The proposed GSS-SPCE framework is applied to the optimal design of buckling-restrained brace cross-sectional areas in a two-story steel building. The objective is to minimize the initial construction cost while satisfying prescribed probabilistic performance constraints. Results show that the proposed framework accurately estimates structural response distributions, including their tail regions, while substantially reducing the number of nonlinear model evaluations required for PBRO.

</details>


### [89] [Canonical Joint Energy-Based Model on CIFAR-10: failure modes and practical indistinguishability of Predictor-Corrector and SGLD samplers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05025)
*Dmytro Knopov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对联合能量模型（JEM）进行了系统的可复现性测试，将理论上更优的Predictor-Corrector (PC)采样器与经典的SGLD采样器在WideResNet-28-10上进行了对比。结果显示PC并未带来任何方法层面的优势，在各协议上两种采样器表现几乎相同，且理论预期上PC在固定噪声下的优势会退化，因此二者实际不可区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEM将分类与生成统一在单一网络中并支持OOD检测，其经典训练依赖SGLD采样器。PC采样器在理论上被证明优于SGLD，但从未在经典JEM模型上经过系统的复现测试。本文旨在填补这一空白，验证PC理论优势在经典JEM实际训练中是否成立。

Method: 在无归一化层的WideResNet-28-10上独立运行两次经典JEM，并在三种协议下对比PC与SGLD：(1) 在约130个训练轮次中全程用PC替换SGLD；(2) 冷启动生成（FID指标）；(3) 精细化多OOD检测（AUROC指标）。使用分层种子级-图像级bootstrap计算95%置信区间，并辅以种子级等价性检验。

Result: 复现结果达到92.88%测试准确率（规范为92.9%）和buffer-FID 44.46（规范为38.40）。发现两个失败模式：晚期训练通过规范离群缓冲机制发生灾难性发散（SGLD与PC均出现），以及依赖运行次数的SVHN OOD判别动态。在所有协议上PC相对SGLD无方法级优势：推理时AUROC绝对差异低于0.007，FID差异低于0.5；训练协议上的宏平均AUROC差异的95%置信区间包含零。

Conclusion: PC与SGLD在经典JEM上实际不可区分，这与理论预期一致：在固定噪声下PC的预测器步骤在构造上退化，其理论保证无法迁移到经典JEM。数据同时与"等价"和"存在微小方向性效应"两种解释一致，但在两个运行样本下无法建立正式的统计等价性。

Abstract: Joint Energy-Based Models (JEM) unify classification and generation within a single network and support out-of-distribution (OOD) detection. Canonical JEM training relies on stochastic gradient Langevin dynamics (SGLD); a theoretically motivated alternative, the Predictor-Corrector (PC) sampler, has not previously undergone a systematic replication test on the canonical model. We reproduce canonical JEM on WideResNet-28-10 without normalisation layers on two independent runs and test whether PC retains its theoretical advantage without an annealed noise schedule, across three protocols: PC replacing SGLD throughout the roughly 130 training epochs; cold-start generation (FID); and refinement-style multi-OOD detection (AUROC). The reconstruction reaches 92.88% test accuracy and buffer-FID 44.46 (canonical: 92.9% and 38.40). We document two failure modes: catastrophic late-training divergence via the canonical outlier-buffer mechanism (both SGLD runs and, with the same signature, both PC runs), and run-dependent SVHN OOD-discrimination dynamics. No method-level advantage of PC over SGLD is observed on any protocol: at inference the absolute AUROC difference stays below 0.007 across all ten checkpoint-OOD pairs and the FID difference below 0.5; on the training protocol a hierarchical seed-by-image bootstrap gives a 95% confidence interval on the macro-averaged AUROC difference that contains zero, while a seed-level equivalence test with two runs per method cannot establish formal equivalence. The data are consistent both with equivalence and with a small directional effect. This practical indistinguishability is theoretically expected: under fixed noise the PC predictor step degenerates by construction, so its guarantees do not transfer to canonical JEM.

</details>


### [90] [Optimizing What Policies Learn From: Recoverability-aware Rollout Intervention Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05080)
*Zheyuan Zhang,Manqing Mao,Hong Wang,Zhuoer Wang,Samson Koelle,Jie Yuan,Yanjun Lin,James Feng,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Yanfang Ye,Wei Niu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RAIL（可恢复性感知的干预学习）框架，将强化学习中的rollout生成视为在线上下文赌博机问题，通过学习在每个干预点生成rollout的收益来决定如何分配有限的计算预算，从而提升大语言模型后训练的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于群体的无评论家强化学习在训练大语言模型时，对每个任务和轨迹状态分配相同数量的rollout，忽略了不同rollout包含的学习信号价值差异。已有自适应方法存在两个局限：(1) 基于固定启发式规则，无法随策略变化而调整；(2) 只决定生成多少rollout，不控制在哪里以及如何干预。

Method: RAIL将干预选择建模为在线上下文赌博机问题，通过shadow-to-live程序收集干预轨迹来训练一个可恢复性控制器。该控制器能够根据每个干预产生的改进信号决定如何生成rollout，并在底层策略演化过程中持续学习更新。

Result: 在有效性、适应性、表达力和效率等多个维度上对RAIL进行了评估。在多种设置下，RAIL在有限的rollout预算下持续提升了性能，表明可恢复性感知的干预能够生成更具信息量、更少冗余的rollout。

Conclusion: 可恢复性感知干预为在训练后阶段生成更有信息量、更少冗余的rollout提供了一种原则性方法，从而产生更强的学习信号。该方法有效克服了现有方法中固定启发式和缺乏干预位置控制的两大局限。

Abstract: Critic-free group-based reinforcement learning has become a scalable approach for post-training large language models. However, most existing methods allocate the same number of rollouts to every task and trajectory state, even though some rollouts provide much more useful learning signals than others. Recent work has started to treat rollout generation as an adaptive decision, but two important limitations remain. First, intervention strategies are often based on fixed heuristics and therefore cannot adjust as the policy changes during training. Second, these methods usually decide only how many rollouts to generate, without explicitly controlling where and how to intervene. To address these limitations, we propose Recoverability-Aware Intervention Learning (RAIL), a training-time framework that learns how to generate rollouts based on the improvement produced by each intervention. RAIL models intervention selection as an online contextual-bandit problem and trains a recoverability controller using intervention traces collected through a shadow-to-live procedure. This allows the controller to keep learning while the underlying policy evolves. We evaluate RAIL in terms of effectiveness, adaptivity, expressiveness, and efficiency. Across multiple settings, RAIL consistently improves performance under limited rollout budgets. These results show that recoverability-aware intervention provides a principled way to generate more informative and less redundant rollouts, leading to stronger learning signals during post-training.

</details>


### [91] [Learning When to Stop: Prefix-Optimal Dynamic Diffusion Policies for Continuous Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05084)
*Rohit Kumar Salla,Manoj Saravanan,Simon Stepputtis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: POGP框架通过学习去噪链上每一步的前缀价值函数，实现自适应早期停止，将扩散策略所需的去噪迭代次数减少约2.7倍，同时保持近满任务性能，并在12个基线的对比中将最终任务性能提升约3.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略在连续控制中表现强大，但其迭代去噪过程计算开销巨大。现有方法通常对所有动作统一采用相同的去噪步数，缺乏根据动作难度自适应调整步数的机制，导致计算资源浪费。

Method: 引入Prefix-Optimal Generative Policies (POGP)框架：通过在去噪链上建立Bellman式递归学习每个中间去噪步骤的前缀价值函数。该函数有双重作用：(1)作为辅助训练目标，鼓励中间输出成为高质量动作；(2)作为测试时停止规则，当进一步去噪不太可能产生有意义改进时提前终止。

Result: 在4个MuJoCo环境和12个基线的对比中，POGP将所需去噪迭代次数减少约2.7倍，同时保留近满任务性能。与最先进的动态扩散基线相比，前缀训练还使最终任务性能提升约3.5%。

Conclusion: 监督中间去噪步骤不仅有助于自适应早期停止，还能作为辅助目标改善学习到的策略本身，验证了前缀训练方法的双重价值。

Abstract: Diffusion policies are a powerful policy class for continuous control, but their iterative denoising process creates a substantial computational bottleneck. Reducing this cost requires adapting the number of denoising steps to the difficulty of each action while preserving task performance. We introduce Prefix-Optimal Generative Policies (POGP), a framework that learns a prefix value function at every intermediate denoising step through a Bellman-style recursion over the denoising chain. The prefix value function serves two purposes: it provides an auxiliary training objective that encourages intermediate outputs to become high-quality actions, and it enables a test-time stopping rule that terminates denoising when additional steps are unlikely to produce meaningful improvement. Across four MuJoCo environments and comparisons with 12 baselines, POGP reduces the required number of denoising iterations by approximately 2.7-fold while retaining near-full task performance. Compared with state-of-the-art dynamic diffusion baselines, prefix training also improves final task performance by approximately 3.5%. These results indicate that supervising intermediate denoising steps is useful not only for adaptive early stopping, but also as an auxiliary objective that improves the learned policy.

</details>


### [92] [MALT: Lightweight Curvature-Aware Muon via Diagonal Preconditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05088)
*Tongle Wu,Huanyu Dong,Ying Sun,Ziye Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MALT（Muon Augmented by Lightweight Two-sided Preconditioning），通过在Muon优化器中引入轻量级双侧对角预条件子来显式捕获损失曲面的曲率几何，以解决Muon对曲率各向异性敏感的问题；并进一步提出MALTER（MALT with Adaptive stEpsize Rescaling）增强对随机梯度噪声的鲁棒性。在GPT-2 Small/Medium/Large预训练实验中，MALT/MALTER在几乎不增加内存和计算开销的情况下超越Muon。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon通过Newton-Schulz迭代对动量矩阵进行正交化来缓解梯度各向异性，在语言模型预训练中已成为AdamW的有力替代，但它并未显式考虑损失曲面的曲率几何，可能对曲率各向异性仍然敏感，因此需要一种轻量级机制来显式捕获曲率信息。

Method: MALT使用双侧对角预条件子以极低的内存和计算开销近似捕获损失曲面的曲率几何；对预条件后的动量通过Newton-Schulz迭代进行正交化，再映射回更新方向；使用norm grafting控制更新幅值。MALTER进一步引入自适应步长缩放以提升对随机梯度噪声的鲁棒性，并在随机非凸设定下提供了收敛性保证。

Result: 在GPT-2 Small、Medium和Large的预训练实验中，MALT和MALTER均优于Muon，同时保持了几乎相同的内存占用和墙钟时间开销。

Conclusion: 轻量级双侧预条件能够有效弥补Muon在曲率几何建模上的不足，在不显著增加开销的前提下提升优化器在大规模语言模型预训练中的性能。

Abstract: Muon has recently emerged as a promising alternative to AdamW for language model pretraining by orthogonalizing momentum matrices using Newton-Schulz iterations. Although Muon mitigates gradient anisotropy, it does not explicitly account for the curvature geometry of the loss landscape and may therefore remain sensitive to curvature anisotropy. We bridge this gap by proposing MALT (Muon Augmented by Lightweight Two-sided Preconditioning), which uses lightweight diagonal preconditioners to reduce the sensitivity of Muon to curvature anisotropy. Specifically, MALT uses two-sided diagonal preconditioners with low memory and computational overhead to approximately capture the curvature geometry of the loss landscape. It orthogonalizes the preconditioned momentum using Newton-Schulz iterations and maps the result back to define the update direction, while norm grafting controls the update magnitude. To improve the robustness of MALT to stochastic gradient noise, we further propose MALTER (MALT with Adaptive stEpsize Rescaling). Convergence guarantees are provided for MALT in the stochastic non-convex setting. Experiments on GPT-2 Small, Medium, and Large pretraining show that the proposed methods outperform Muon while maintaining nearly the same memory footprint and wall-clock time.

</details>


### [93] [Multimodal Spatiotemporal Atmospheric Data Assimilation with Latent Flow-matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05103)
*Dibyajyoti Chakraborty,Romit Maulik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于潜在视频流匹配的大气数据同化统一方法，利用ERA5再分析数据训练的先验模型生成时间一致性轨迹，通过后验采样同化真实观测数据，可统一处理滤波和平滑任务，并直接从稀疏观测生成集合预报，性能与最先进观测-预报模型相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据同化方法依赖贝叶斯推理更新数值预报模型状态，缺乏统一框架来处理不同同化任务（如滤波和平滑）。本文旨在提出一种根本不同的、统一的大气数据同化方法，以简化并提升同化效率。

Method: 采用潜在视频流匹配（latent video flow-matching）方法，从ERA5再分析数据（8天窗口、69个变量）中训练先验模型，生成时间一致的轨迹；通过后验采样同化来自NOAA集成全球无线电探空档案和集成地表数据库的真实观测数据。

Result: 该先验生成的连续轨迹可自然地在观测和非观测帧之间传播信息，仅通过改变观测帧即可完成滤波和平滑等不同数据同化任务；此外，可直接从稀疏观测生成全状态集合预报，性能与最先进的观测到预报模型相当。

Conclusion: 本文展示了基于生成模型的无缝统一数据同化框架，兼顾了滤波、平滑和集合预报等多项任务，同时验证了该方法在真实观测数据上的有效性。

Abstract: Data assimilation (DA) uses Bayesian inference to update the state of a numerical forecast model with observed data. In this study, we propose a fundamentally different, unified approach to atmospheric data assimilation. We use latent video flow-matching to sample temporally consistent trajectories from a prior trained using ERA5 reanalysis (69 variables over an 8-day window). We also use posterior sampling to assimilate real observation sources, such as those from the NOAA Integrated Global Radiosonde Archive and the Integrated Surface Database. Because the prior generates a continuous trajectory, it naturally propagates information between observed and unobserved frames. Therefore, we can perform various DA tasks, such as filtering and smoothing, simply by changing the observed frames. Moreover, we generate full-state ensemble forecasts directly from sparse observations, achieving performance competitive with state-of-the-art observation-to-forecast models.

</details>


### [94] [BnBERT-iPET: Sparse Few-Shot Language Modeling for Bengali via Lottery Ticket Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05104)
*Sajib Hossain,Md Kamrus Samad,Anan Ghosh,Labib Imam Chowdhury,Nabeel Mohammed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BnBERT-iPET，一种针对孟加拉语的稀疏少样本语言建模方法。通过迭代模式利用训练（iPET）进行少样本学习，并利用彩票假设（Lottery Ticket Hypothesis）剪枝技术实现90%稀疏度（仅保留原始模型10%的边），实验表明该轻量级模型在下游任务上可与Bangla Electra、Indic-BERT和XLM-RoBERTa等大型模型竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型预训练模型（如BERT）在资源受限的孟加拉语场景中训练成本高昂、碳排放量大、计算资源需求高。复杂神经网络中并非所有边都同等重要，剪枝技术可以在保持性能的同时减少内存占用、缩短训练时间并提升推理效率，因此有必要探索面向资源受限语言的稀疏少样本方法。

Method: 1）提出BnBERT-iPET模型，结合iPET（迭代模式利用训练）实现少样本学习；2）利用彩票假设（Lottery Ticket Hypothesis）剪枝技术，将原始BERT模型的边剪至仅剩10%（即90%稀疏度）；3）在孟加拉语标准基准数据集上进行下游任务评估。

Result: 仅保留10%边的BnBERT-iPET模型在孟加拉语下游任务上与更大的模型（如Bangla Electra、Indic-BERT和XLM-RoBERTa）表现不相上下，证明了高稀疏度剪枝结合少样本学习可以在资源受限语言场景中达到近乎顶尖的性能。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过iPET少样本训练和彩票假设剪枝相结合，可以构建轻量级、高效的稀疏语言模型，在资源受限的低资源语言（如孟加拉语）上实现与大规模模型竞争的性能，为资源受限环境下的NLP模型部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Deep neural networks have shown impressive success in NLP tasks owing to their complex structure and huge number of edges. Achieving state-of-the-art performance in natural language processing with a large pre-trained model such as BERT is expensive and time-consuming, carries a large carbon footprint, and is difficult to realize on machines with minimal computational capability. This creates a barrier to training complex models for resource-constrained languages such as Bengali. However, in a complex neural model, not all edges are equally impactful, and the contributions of some of them can be neglected. Pruning promises to reduce the memory footprint of regular networks, shorten the training time of ever-growing networks, and increase inference efficiency without sacrificing comparable performance. In this work, we introduce BnBERT-iPET, a sparse few-shot language modeling approach for Bengali, and experimentally show that a lightweight few-shot-learned language model retaining only 10% of the edges of an initial model such as BERT can perform neck and neck with much larger models on challenging tasks for a resource-constrained language such as Bengali. By learning from few shots through iterative pattern exploiting training and achieving 90% sparsity with the Lottery Ticket Hypothesis pruning technique, our pruned BnBERT-iPET model proves to be a tough competitor to state-of-the-art language models such as Bangla Electra, Indic-BERT, and XLM-RoBERTa on downstream tasks over standard benchmark datasets of the Bengali language.

</details>


### [95] [Reward Structure Shapes the Interaction Between Episodic Exploration and Neural Memory in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05111)
*Jai Malegaonkar,Rohan Patil,Henrik I. Christensen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实验系统研究了部分可观测强化学习中探索奖金与记忆架构之间的交互作用，发现相同的奖金信号在不同环境下产生三种截然不同的交互模式（放大架构差异、拉平架构差异、完全无效），并提出了观测锚定奖励机的形式化框架来区分结构性稀疏与潜在稀疏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上探索奖金和记忆架构被孤立地评估，二者之间的交互作用未被测量；此外，标准稀疏奖励的概念将时间信号密度与奖励实际监督的内容混为一谈，缺乏对奖励结构稀疏性的精确形式化。

Method: 采用受控实验设计，在三个奖励内容获取方式不同的环境中交叉组合情节探索奖金与多种神经记忆架构；通过受控奖励操作（密集奖励中和奖金、小额可避免惩罚）验证交互模式是否跟踪奖励结构而非密度；最后提出观测锚定奖励机（observation-anchored reward machines）来形式化区分结构性稀疏与潜在稀疏。

Result: 相同的奖金信号产生三种交互模式：在需要主动发现并无人监督留存的记忆内容环境中放大架构能力差异；在单一奖励监督线索场景中使各架构收敛到共享上限；在纯调度观测流场景中奖金完全无效。密集奖励仅在直接监督所需潜在记忆时才能中和奖金；小额可避免惩罚会诱导策略收敛到次优驻留状态，且两种奖金均可解决该问题。

Conclusion: 探索与记忆是互补而非替代关系：探索奖金负责制造暴露（exposure），而记忆架构负责将暴露转化为回报。观测锚定奖励机的形式化词汇通过每个任务暴露的记忆留存负担来组织三种交互模式。

Abstract: In partially observable reinforcement learning, agents face a dual bottleneck: they must explore to encounter rewarding states and retain that experience in memory to optimize their policies. Exploration bonuses and memory architectures are traditionally evaluated in isolation, leaving their interaction unmeasured, and standard notions of sparse reward conflate temporal signal density with what the reward actually supervises. We present a controlled study crossing episodic exploration bonuses with diverse neural memory architectures across three environments that vary how the content of memory is acquired. An identical bonus signal yields three distinct interaction patterns: it amplifies architectural capacity differences where memory content must be actively discovered and retained unsupervised; equalizes architectures to a shared ceiling where the content, once sought out, is a single reward-supervised cue; and is null where the observation stream is purely scheduled. Controlled reward manipulations verify that these patterns track reward structure rather than density: a dense reward neutralizes a bonus only if it directly supervises the required latent memory, and a small avoidable penalty on exploratory actions (leaving the optimum unchanged) induces policy convergence to suboptimal stationary states, which either bonus resolves. We then formalize reward sparsity with observation-anchored reward machines, separating structural sparsity (an automaton reproduces the return without the task-required history) from potential sparsity (the one-step reward misprices local exploratory actions); the resulting vocabulary organizes the three regimes by the retention burden each task exposes. Together, these results show exploration and memory are complements, not substitutes: a bonus induces exposure, and only memory converts exposure into return.

</details>


### [96] [DASyR-LLM: Domain-Aware Symbolic Regression with LLMs for Kinetic Model Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05120)
*Roberto Aliaga Medina,Paulina Quintanilla,Antonio del Rio Chanona*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个LLM引导的符号回归（SR）框架，将大型语言模型嵌入迭代SR算法中，用于自动发现动力学模型。LLM在每轮迭代中扮演两个角色：对最佳SR候选模型进行定性物理化学批判，以及基于SR生成模型和化学知识提出新的候选速率表达式。在四个虚拟案例（涵盖多相催化和生物过程）中，该框架将识别真实模型的迭代次数减少了41.7%-79.3%，并在超过一半的引导实验中直接提出正确模型结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号回归虽能识别可解释的动力学模型，但通常不结合领域知识，往往探索物理化学上不合理的模型。LLM为将领域专业知识注入搜索过程提供了途径，有望提升动力学模型发现的效率和物理合理性。

Method: 框架将LLM模块嵌入迭代SR算法中。每轮迭代中，LLM执行两个任务：(1) 对最优SR候选模型进行定性物理化学批判；(2) 依据SR生成的模型和化学知识提出新的候选速率表达式。通过四个复杂度递增的虚拟案例（涉及多相催化和生物过程系统）评估，并进行消融研究分析SR组件和LLM规模的影响。

Result: LLM引导框架相比最先进的SR框架，识别真实模型的迭代次数减少41.7%-79.3%，且超过一半的引导运行中LLM直接提出正确模型结构。独立验证集的预测性能两者相当（所有案例R²>0.98）。消融研究表明SR组件和LLM规模均对性能有贡献，小型LLM仍能保持大部分发现效率。

Conclusion: LLM能够有效将领域知识注入科学模型发现过程，为完全自动化、领域感知的动力学建模流程铺平了道路。在实际应用中将显著减少湿实验迭代次数，降低实验成本。

Abstract: Kinetic model discovery is a central challenge in chemical engineering, as accurate rate expressions are essential for understanding and controlling chemical and biological processes. Symbolic regression (SR) has emerged as a powerful data-driven approach for identifying interpretable kinetic models, but usually operates without domain knowledge, often exploring physicochemically implausible models. Large language models (LLMs) offer a promising avenue for injecting domain expertise into this search. Here, we introduce an LLM-guided SR framework, embedding an LLM module within an iterative SR algorithm for automated kinetic model discovery. The LLM performs two roles at each iteration: (1) a qualitative physicochemical critique of the best SR candidates, and (2) the proposal of new candidate rate expressions guided by the SR-generated models and embedded chemical knowledge. Our framework is evaluated on four in silico case studies of increasing complexity, spanning heterogeneous catalysis and bioprocess systems. Results show the LLM-guided framework reduces iterations to identify the ground-truth model by $41.7-79.3\%$ versus a state-of-the-art SR framework, with the LLM directly proposing the correct model structure in over half of the guided runs. In practical settings, where each iteration typically requires a new wet-lab experiment, this translates into a substantial reduction in experimental effort. Predictive performance on an independent validation set is equivalent between both approaches, with $R^2>0.98$ in all case studies. Ablation studies indicate that both the SR component and the LLM scale contribute to this performance, with a reduced-size LLM largely retaining discovery efficiency. These findings demonstrate that LLMs can effectively inject domain knowledge into scientific model discovery, paving the way toward fully automated, domain-aware kinetic modelling pipelines.

</details>


### [97] [The Loss Does Not See the Basis, but Adam Does](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05136)
*Devender Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一个核心发现：对于分解模型 W=UV^T，梯度下降隐式偏向低秩解，而 Adam 从相同小初始化出发则不具备此性质。根源在于损失函数在 (U,V)→(UQ,VQ) 规范变换下的不变性（规范对称性）。只有满足规范等变条件的优化器才能继承梯度流的低秩机制，但等变性只是必要条件而非充分条件。梯度下降、动量、共享标量 Adam、Muon 和 Shampoo 满足等变性；Adam、RMSProp 及坐标级方法不满足。结构定理证明无记忆等变规则恰好等价于 Gram 决定的左预条件器，转移定理将梯度流的路径性质推广到共同标量流。作者在欠定矩阵感知任务上对九种更新规则排序，发现从坐标级到共享标量预条件的单参数族能单调恢复低秩偏置，证明各向异性是根因。"谱调度"调和了关于 Muon 的两份矛盾报告：等速率更新能精确恢复低秩目标，但随谱尾增长优势减弱。在 Transformer 中，Adam 在第一步就分离两种规范等价的初始化（等变优化器在浮点精度内保持一致），最终各头不变量 W_Q^T W_K 相对 Frobenius 距离相差 56%，该差距无法通过逐头旋转消除。在两个高光谱数据集上，在匹配训练损失时，梯度下降在最稀疏采样密度下将留出误差降低 43-44%，且有效秩更低。因此，基选择（basis choice）不是调参细节，而是决定优化器选择哪个插值点的关键决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解不同优化器（尤其是 Adam 与梯度下降）在低秩矩阵分解问题中隐式偏差差异的根源，并解释为何某些优化器倾向于低秩解而另一些则不然。作者希望从规范和对称性的角度给出统一的理论框架。

Method: 釡用规范对称性（gauge symmetry）分析框架：识别损失在 (UQ,VQ) 变换下的不变性；提出规范等变（gauge-equivariant）作为优化器继承梯度流低秩机制的必要条件；通过结构定理刻画无记忆等变规则（即 Gram 决定的左预条件器）；建立转移定理将梯度流的路径性质推广到共同标量流；通过一参数预条件家族从坐标级连续过渡到共享标量级来系统性验证各向异性作为偏置消失的原因；在矩阵感知、Transformer 和高光谱数据集上做系统实验对比九种更新规则。

Result: （1）梯度下降、动量、共享标量 Adam、Muon、Shampoo 具备规范等变性；Adam、RMSProp 及坐标级方法不具备。（2）规范等变是低秩转移的必要但不充分条件。（3）结构定理：无记忆等变规则恰好是 Gram 决定的左预条件器。（4）一参数族从坐标级到共享标量级单调恢复低秩偏置，证实各向异性是根源。（5）谱调度解释了 Muon 的矛盾结果：等速率更新精确恢复低秩目标，谱尾增长时优势消失。（6）Adam 在 Transformer 中第一步就分离规范等价初始化，最终各头 W_Q^T W_K 相对距离 56%。（7）高光谱实验中梯度下降在匹配训练损失下留出误差降低 43-44%，有效秩更低。

Conclusion: 优化器的基选择（basis choice）是一个关于模型选择的根本决策：规范等变优化器继承了梯度流对低秩解的隐式偏置，而坐标级各向异性优化器（如 Adam）则不具备这一性质。这意味着在深度学习中常用的"预处理不影响最终解"的直觉是错误的——预条件算子决定了优化器在参数空间中选取的插值点，进而影响泛化性能。
