Abstract: Gradient descent on a factored model $W = UV^\top$ is implicitly biased toward low-rank solutions, while Adam, starting from the same small initialization, is not. We trace the difference to the gauge symmetry of the loss, its invariance under $(U, V) \mapsto (UQ, VQ)$. Gradient flow's low-rank mechanism is available to an optimizer only if that optimizer is gauge-equivariant, a condition necessary for the transfer but not sufficient for low-rank recovery. Gradient descent, momentum, "shared-scalar" Adam, Muon, and Shampoo satisfy it. Adam, RMSProp, and the other coordinate-wise methods do not. A structure theorem characterizes the memoryless equivariant rules as exactly the Gram-determined left preconditioners, and a transfer theorem carries gradient flow's pathwise properties to common-scalar flows. We then sort nine update rules on underdetermined matrix sensing by recovery error against the planted ground truth. A one-parameter family from coordinate-wise to shared-scalar preconditioning restores the bias monotonically, isolating anisotropy as the cause. A "spectral schedule" reconciles two opposing reports about Muon: equal-rate updates recover exactly low-rank targets but lose their edge as the spectral tail grows. In transformers, Adam separates two gauge-equivalent initializations at the first step, where the equivariant optimizers stay at float precision, and ends with the per-head invariants $W_Q^\top W_K$ 56% apart in relative Frobenius distance, a gap no per-head rotation can close. On two hyperspectral datasets at matched training loss, gradient descent cuts held-out error by 43-44% at the lowest sampling density, and at lower effective rank. Basis choice is therefore not a tuning detail but a decision about which interpolant the optimizer selects.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [98] [A Distributed Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm For Unit Commitment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04159)
*Ali Rajabi,Milad Hasanzadeh,Amin Kargarian*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分布式量子近似优化算法（DQAOA）的三块交替方向乘子法（ADMM）框架，用于求解机组组合（UC）问题。该框架将松弛的连续变量在二次规划块中求解，将二进制机组启停变量建模为QUBO问题，并支持暴力枚举、单片QAOA和分布式QAOA三种求解模式，在五机组算例中验证了三种模式结果的一致性及多QPU容量扩展能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机组组合（UC）问题包含混合整数规划，二进制变量求解困难。随着系统规模扩大，经典求解方法的计算瓶颈日益突出；同时，单一量子处理单元（QPU）容量受限于逻辑量子比特数量，无法承载完整的二进制问题。因此需要一种能够跨多个QPU分配量子比特的分布式量子算法框架来缓解容量约束。

Method: 构建基于DQAOA的ADMM三块分解框架：1）连续机组启停与发电调度变量在连续二次规划块中求解；2）二进制机组启停变量被建模为QUBO问题，通过DQAOA接口实现求解；3）DQAOA接口支持三种模式——暴力枚举、单片QAOA和分布式QAOA，分布式模式下将逻辑量子比特分配到多个容量受限的QPU上运行；4）其余ADMM更新步骤保持不变。

Result: 在一个包含15个二进制变量的五机组UC算例上进行评估。三种求解模式均能将ADMM原始残差降至容忍阈值以下，并获得相同的机组启停计划、调度结果和运行成本。结果表明三种求解模式解的一致性，且通过分布式QAOA实现了多QPU容量扩展能力。

Conclusion: 该DQAOA-ADMM框架为机组组合问题提供了一种可扩展的量子计算求解途径，在保证解一致性的同时，通过多QPU分配突破了单片量子设备容量限制，为未来大规模UC问题的量子求解奠定了框架基础。

Abstract: This paper presents a distributed quantum approximate optimization algorithm (DQAOA)-enabled three-block alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) framework for unit commitment (UC). The relaxed commitment and dispatch variables are solved in a continuous quadratic programming block, while the binary commitment block is formulated as a quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) problem. The DQAOA interface allows this QUBO to be solved using brute-force enumeration, monolithic QAOA, or distributed QAOA, while the remaining ADMM updates are kept unchanged. In the distributed mode, the logical commitment qubits are allocated across multiple capacity-constrained quantum processing units (QPU), avoiding the requirement that the complete binary problem fits on a single device. The framework is evaluated on a five-unit UC instance containing 15 binary variables. All three solver modes reduce the ADMM primal residual below a certain tolerance and recover the same commitment schedule, dispatch, and operating cost. The results demonstrate solution consistency across the three solver modes and the multi-QPU capacity accommodation provided by the distributed QAOA method.

</details>


### [99] [Zero-Instrumentation Dependency Discovery for Guided Microservice Migration Using eBPF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04413)
*Eshan Trivedi,Chandrahasa Pranava*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出利用eBPF内核级网络追踪自动发现微服务间运行时依赖关系，免于应用插桩，并据此生成按ROI排序的流量感知迁移计划。在20服务测试台中，系统从13,615个网络事件中恢复出32条依赖边，识别出全部20个服务；迁移排序将跨VM流量暴露降低27%，但近饱和负载下采集开销显著（p99延迟上升1,050%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务在虚拟机间迁移时，若缺乏对运行时通信模式的了解，仅靠静态分析难以预测跨VM热点和延迟尖峰，因此需要一种无需应用改造即能自动发现服务依赖、并据此优化迁移计划的方法。

Method: 采用eBPF内核级网络追踪自动发现运行时服务间依赖（无需应用插桩），构建依赖图；通过两阶段PID到端口的关联算法恢复共享运行时测试台中进程身份；以ROI排序迁移计划，并采用谱聚类配合Kernighan-Lin精化将服务划分为VM内聚群组。

Result: 在20服务测试台中，系统用13,615个网络事件（3分钟捕获）发现32条依赖边，准确匹配地面真值拓扑；ROI排序迁移相比字母序代理基线降低27%的迁移窗口累计跨VM流量暴露。但采集开销明显：近饱和负载下吞吐仅降4.4%，而p50延迟上升383%、p99延迟上升1,050%。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效自动发现微服务依赖并生成流量感知迁移计划，但eBPF采集在饱和负载下延迟开销严重，建议在非高峰时段或专用采样节点上运行捕获；且结论仅基于单一20服务测试台，不宣称在真实生产依赖图上的普适性。

Abstract: Migrating microservices across virtual machines (VMs) without knowledge of their runtime communication patterns risks creating cross-VM hotspots and latency spikes that are difficult to predict from static analysis alone. We use extended Berkeley Packet Filter (eBPF) kernel-level network tracing to automatically discover inter-service dependencies at runtime, with no application instrumentation, and use the resulting dependency graph to produce a traffic-aware migration plan ranked by return on investment (ROI). A two-pass process-identifier (PID) to port correlation algorithm recovers the identity of all 20 services in a shared-runtime testbed where processes are otherwise indistinguishable, matching the known ground-truth topology. The system discovers 32 dependency edges from 13,615 network events captured in three minutes, and applies spectral graph clustering with Kernighan-Lin refinement to partition services into VM-coherent groups. In simulation over the discovered graph, our ROI-ranked migration order reduces cumulative cross-VM traffic exposure during the migration window by 27% relative to alphabetical ordering, a deterministic proxy for arbitrary dependency-blind ordering. Collection overhead is mixed: in a controlled A/B test at near-saturation load on a host with two virtual CPUs (vCPUs), throughput fell by only 4.4%, but median (p50) latency rose by 383% and 99th-percentile (p99) latency by 1,050%. We therefore recommend running captures off-peak or on dedicated sampling nodes rather than under production saturation. All results are from a single 20-service testbed that we authored; we make no claim about behavior on production dependency graphs.

</details>


### [100] [CommBench: Can LLMs Write Correct and Efficient GPU Communication Code?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04450)
*Shuang Ma,Yuyi Li,Yihan Zhang,Hezhi Xie,Danyang Chen,Shuyang Ji,Ziming Mao,Cheng Ji,Ansha Prashanth,Wenting Yang,Yiran Wang,Chihan Cui,Pei Yu Lin,Ion Stoica,Yang Zhou*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出CommBench，一个包含100多个专家精心设计的GPU通信编程综合基准测试，用于评估代码生成大语言模型在GPU通信编程方面的能力。评估发现最强的GPT-5.5模型也仅能在30.7%的任务上正确实现并达到竞争性性能，揭示了当前LLM与专家级GPU通信代码之间的巨大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的训练和推理高度依赖高性能GPU通信，但编写高效的GPU通信原语需要深厚的GPU架构、网络硬件和分布式通信模式专业知识，这对代码生成模型极具挑战性。目前缺乏一个专门针对这一领域的综合性评估基准。

Method: CommBench包含超过100个专家精心设计的任务，覆盖点对点通信、集合通信、专家并行通信、计算-通信融合和通信工具函数五个类别。参考实现由GPU通信专家编写或从生产代码库中提炼。同时引入防作弊评估框架，自动在多GPU系统上编译、执行和验证生成代码，并使用统一指标联合衡量功能正确性和通信性能。实验在节点内NVLink和节点间RDMA两种平台上进行。

Result: 评估结果显示，包括GPT-5.5在内的前沿和开源代码生成模型，在CommBench上最强的GPT-5.5也仅能在30.7%的任务上正确实现并达到竞争性性能，表明当前LLM与专家编写的GPU通信代码之间存在显著差距。

Conclusion: CommBench揭示了大语言模型在GPU通信编程方面的显著不足，为该领域设立了具有挑战性的基准，为推进AI辅助系统编程提供了重要的评估工具。

Abstract: Training and serving large language models (LLMs) rely heavily on high-performance GPU communication, yet implementing efficient GPU communication primitives requires deep expertise in GPU architectures, networking hardware, and distributed communication patterns, making them particularly challenging for code generation models. We present CommBench, a comprehensive benchmark for GPU communication programming, consisting of over 100 expert-curated tasks spanning point-to-point communication, collective operations, expert-parallel communication, compute--communication fusion, and communication utility functions, with reference implementations either written by GPU communication experts or distilled from production codebases. We further introduce a cheat-resistant evaluation framework that automatically compiles, executes, and validates generated code on multi-GPU systems, and a unified metric that jointly measures functional correctness and communication performance. Evaluating leading frontier and open-source code generation models on both intra-node NVLink and inter-node RDMA platforms reveals that even the strongest model, GPT-5.5, correctly implements and achieves competitive performance on only 30.7\% of the benchmark tasks. Our results expose a substantial gap between current LLMs and expert-written GPU communication code, establishing CommBench as a challenging benchmark for advancing AI-assisted systems programming.

</details>


### [101] [AFD-Ledger: Deployment Provisioning for Attention--FFN Disaggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04502)
*Chengyu Qiu,Xiao Fu,Fengcun Li,Yulei Qian,Yuchen Xie,Xunliang Cai,Yingdi Shan,Yongwei Wu,Mingxing Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出AFD-Ledger，一个离线分析型供给系统，用于在给定模型、工作负载、SLO、硬件预算和运行时能力的前提下，计算AFD与最优化collocated部署（MoE服务）之间的吞吐量差异，以回答"AFD是否优于最优collocated部署"这一部署问题。AFD-Ledger通过分析型执行模型与评估边界硬件搜索来降低供给开销，并在多种配置下验证了其准确性和部署决策的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AFD作为服务MoE语言模型的新兴架构虽有潜力，但现有AFD系统未回答一个关键部署问题：在相同条件下（同一模型、工作负载、TPOT SLO、硬件预算等），AFD是否真的比最优的collocated部署提供更高吞吐量？回答该问题需要对两种架构联合优化硬件分配与部署组织，而穷举式供给成本过高。因此需要一个低开销且准确的供给系统来系统性地回答该问题。

Method: AFD-Ledger采用离线分析型供给方法：（1）使用分析型执行模型对AFD和collocated部署分别建模；（2）使用评估边界硬件搜索（evaluation-bounded hardware search）在硬件分配空间中高效搜索，避免穷举；（3）独立为两种架构进行供给，并在模型吞吐量预测上做验证，最终确定在给定预算和硬件目录下哪种部署组织更优。

Result: 在可穷举部署空间内，AFD-Ledger减少了68.8%--83.5%的完整部署评估量，同时仍能恢复全局最优部署。在三个物理LongCat 2.0部署上，它正确保持了架构决策，并将AFD对collocated的吞吐量预测误差控制在6.6%--9.6%以内。基于该验证框架的发现：同构AFD仅在少数设置中提升固定预算吞吐量；异构AFD需要部署级别的硬件互补性而非启发式设备选择；角色特化硬件改进的价值主要在于跨过部署能力-价格边界以实现更好的部署组织。

Conclusion: AFD-Ledger提供了一个高精度、低开销的分析型供给框架，能够对AFD与collocated两种MoE部署架构做出正确的架构级决策。通过该框架揭示：AFD并非对所有配置都优于collocated部署；异构AFD的关键在于部署级别的硬件互补性；硬件改进的意义取决于其能否促成更好的部署组织而非单纯提升单设备能力。

Abstract: Attention--Feed-Forward Network (FFN) Disaggregation (AFD) is emerging as a promising architecture for serving Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models. While existing AFD systems improve the efficiency of disaggregated execution, they leave a deployment question unanswered: under the same model, workload, time-per-output-token (TPOT) service-level objective (SLO), hardware budget, hardware catalog, and runtime capabilities, does AFD provide higher throughput than the best collocated deployment? Answering this question requires jointly optimizing hardware assignment and deployment organization for both architectures, making exhaustive provisioning prohibitively expensive. We present AFD-Ledger, an offline analytical provisioning system that independently provisions AFD and collocated deployments using an analytical execution model and an evaluation-bounded hardware search. Across deployment spaces where exhaustive provisioning is feasible, AFD-Ledger reduces complete deployment evaluations by 68.8%--83.5% while still recovering the globally optimal deployment. On three physical LongCat 2.0 deployments, it preserves the correct architecture decision while predicting AFD-to-collocated throughput within 6.6%--9.6% of measurement. Using this validated framework, we show that homogeneous AFD improves fixed-budget throughput in only a minority of the studied settings, heterogeneous AFD requires deployment-level hardware complementarity rather than heuristic device selection, and role-specific hardware improvements matter primarily when they enable better deployment organizations by crossing deployment capability--price boundaries.

</details>


### [102] [AsymSpec: Efficient Cloud-Edge Speculative Decoding over Asymmetric Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04974)
*Guotao Yang,Hao Chen,Rui Guo,Xinyu Li,Liang Zheng,Sheng Chen,Yitao Hu,Keqiu Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: AsymSpec是一种面向云边协同推测解码的异步验证协议与流水线调度方案，通过非对称上下行通信设计和已确认前缀流水线，解决上行受限场景下验证器空闲与无效依赖工作浪费问题，端到端吞吐量提升2.82-28.03倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云边推测解码中，边缘网关部署轻量草稿模型、云端部署高质量目标模型，但每个推测块都引入通信开销。在上行带宽受限条件下，候选消息可能排队而验证器空闲；停止等待调度导致边缘计算资源利用不足，乐观的同请求流水线推进在拒绝或意外奖励token使依赖草稿失效时造成计算浪费。

Method: 提出两种核心机制：(1) 非对称验证协议——保持常见路径的接受上传紧凑，仅将更丰富的拒绝信息通过下行传输；利用残差分布的总变差（TV）证书判断小型目标top-K响应是否足够，若不足则逐步升级至基于提案的精确恢复，最后回退到完整分布。(2) 已确认前缀流水线——仅向边缘调度器暴露独立有效的请求，云端对到达的块进行重新批处理，在另一个已确认前缀请求就绪时隐藏验证等待，不依赖同请求流水线推进。

Result: 在三种草稿-目标模型对、两种工作负载和三种非对称网络配置下的端到端评估显示，AsymSpec的输出token吞吐量是最强基线的2.82-28.03倍。

Conclusion: AsymSpec通过非对称通信协议和已确认前缀流水线的协同设计，有效解决了上行受限场景下云边推测解码的验证瓶颈与无效计算问题，显著提升了端到端吞吐量。

Abstract: Cloud-edge speculative decoding places a lightweight draft model at an edge gateway and a higher-quality target model in the cloud, but inserts communication into every speculative block. Under a constrained uplink, candidate messages may queue while the verifier is idle. Stop-and-wait scheduling leaves edge compute underutilized; optimistic same-request runahead can waste work when a rejection or an unexpected bonus token invalidates dependent drafts. We present AsymSpec, which addresses uplink-gated verification and invalid dependent work with two corresponding mechanisms. Its asymmetric verification protocol keeps the common-path acceptance upload compact and moves richer, rejection-only correction information to the downlink. A total-variation (TV) certificate for the residual distribution determines whether a small target top-K response suffices; if not, the protocol progressively escalates through proposal-based exact recovery before falling back to the full distribution. Its confirmed-prefix pipeline exposes only independent, valid requests to the edge scheduler and lets the cloud re-batch arrived blocks, hiding verification waits when another confirmed-prefix request is ready without using same-request runahead. Across three draft-target pairs, two workloads, and three asymmetric network profiles, our end-to-end evaluation shows that AsymSpec delivers 2.82-28.03$\times$ the output-token throughput of the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [103] [RAC: Reference-Aware Activation Compression for Communication-Efficient Split LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04991)
*Guotao Yang,Mingxi Zhao,Haopeng Li,Zhengchao Wang,Sheng Chen,Yitao Hu,Keqiu Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出RAC（Reference-Aware Codec），一种用于LLM拆分推理（split inference）的参考感知编解码器，通过检索精确token历史片段、复用重建的上行状态以及生成边界特定的解码参考，大幅减少本地-云端-本地路径中边界隐状态传输的通信瓶颈。在三个模型和九组模型-链路组合上，Raw-to-RAC的TTFT和TPOT比值分别达到1.24-2.72倍和1.01-2.79倍，任务分数变化在-0.40到+2.50分之间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent在反复处理长且隐私敏感的上下文时，云端部署会将用户数据暴露在可信端点之外，而完全本地部署又需要昂贵硬件。拆分推理在本地执行模型头部、尾部和工具，云端执行中间层，但其本地-云-本地的路径每次调用都传输边界隐状态，造成严重的通信瓶颈。因此需要一种方法来压缩/编解码这些传输的隐状态，同时保证质量和效率。

Method: RAC包含三个核心组件：(1) 参考感知的prefill上行链路：检索精确token历史跨度来生成参考；(2) prefill下行链路复用重建的上行状态；(3) 解码阶段通过轻量级因果预测器生成边界特定的解码参考。RAC还采用分组仿射对齐（grouped affine alignment）和带可选prefill异常值的校准残差量化（calibrated residual quantization），并通过发送端线格式（wire-format）重建来同步后续参考，离线校准考虑质量和打包表示成本。

Result: 在三个模型和九组模型-链路组合的评估中，Raw-to-RAC的平均TTFT（首token时间）比值和TPOT（每token输出时间）比值分别为1.24-2.72倍和1.01-2.79倍；12个非困惑度任务分数变化范围为-0.40到+2.50分，表明RAC在显著加速通信的同时保持了良好的任务质量。

Conclusion: RAC通过参考感知编解码策略有效解决了拆分推理中的通信瓶颈问题，在保持任务性能的同时显著提升推理效率，为隐私敏感的LLM agent部署提供了一种兼顾本地隐私保护和云端计算资源的实用方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents repeatedly process long, privacy-sensitive contexts, while cloud-only deployment exposes user data beyond the trusted endpoint and fully local deployment often requires costly hardware. Split inference offers a middle ground by executing the model head, tail, and tools locally and the middle layers in the cloud, but its local-cloud-local path transfers boundary hidden states at every invocation and creates a critical communication bottleneck. We present \system, a reference-aware codec that retrieves exact-token historical spans for prefill uplinks, reuses the reconstructed uplink state for same-round prefill downlinks, and generates boundary-specific decode references with lightweight causal predictors. RAC applies grouped affine alignment and calibrated residual quantization with optional prefill outliers, while sender-side wire-format reconstruction synchronizes subsequent references and offline calibration accounts for quality and packed representation costs. Across three models and nine evaluated model-link pairs, Raw-to-RAC mean time to first token (TTFT) and time per output token (TPOT) ratios are 1.24-2.72$\times$ and 1.01-2.79$\times$, while the 12 non-perplexity task-score changes range from $-0.40$ to $+2.50$ points.

</details>


### [104] [SparseDitto: Customizing GPU Kernels for Different Sparsity Patterns with LLM-Based Agentic System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05033)
*Shiyang Li,Guangyan Sun,Jinwei Tang,Yanzhi Wang,Mingyi Hong,Caiwen Ding*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了SparseDitto，一个基于LLM（大语言模型）的系统，能够针对每个稀疏矩阵、算子及目标GPU自动构建优化的GPU内核。该系统在三个稀疏算子和多种矩阵上，相比cuSPARSE实现了2.68x（RTX PRO 6000）和2.79x（H200）的几何平均加速，最大分别达到146.61x和78.5x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏矩阵内核在科学计算、图分析和机器学习中至关重要，但GPU性能高度依赖输入稀疏模式和执行策略。同一矩阵上，cuSPARSE在CSR和Blocked-ELL格式间有350x的性能差距，且现有数据格式、专用系统和稀疏编译器都无法在所有稀疏模式和算子下保持优势。因此需要一个能针对每个工作负载和目标GPU自适应调整表示、执行策略和硬件映射的系统。

Method: SparseDitto采用统一设计框架，支持SpMV、SpMM和SpGEMM三个稀疏算子。其方法包括三层设计：首先，一个轻量级加性模型利用输入矩阵的结构特征对已有策略进行排序；其次，一个架构感知的规划器提出多个候选设计方案；最后，编码和验证智能体（agents）利用目标GPU上的实际测量结果来实现并迭代优化这些方案。整个流程由LLM驱动，为每个矩阵、算子和GPU组合生成定制化的GPU内核。

Result: 在三个稀疏算子和多样化矩阵集合上，SparseDitto在NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 GPU上相对cuSPARSE实现2.68x几何平均加速（最大146.61x），在NVIDIA H200 GPU上实现2.79x几何平均加速（最大78.5x）。其生成的SpMM内核还能将全批次GCN训练加速最多3.39x。

Conclusion: 没有单一实现能在所有稀疏模式和算子上持续占据优势，而基于LLM的自适应内核生成方法能够有效弥合不同格式和策略之间的性能差距。SparseDitto通过为每个具体工作负载定制内核，在多个GPU平台上一致地大幅超越cuSPARSE，并展示了该方法对下游机器学习任务（如GCN训练）的实际加速价值。

Abstract: Sparse matrix kernels are fundamental to scientific computing, graph analytics, and machine learning. Their GPU performance depends strongly on the input sparsity pattern and execution strategy. For the same SpMM on the same matrix, cuSPARSE exhibits a 350x performance gap between CSR and Blocked-ELL. Our study of multiple data formats, specialized systems, and sparse compilers shows that no single implementation consistently dominates across sparsity patterns and operators. This motivates a system that can adapt its representation, execution strategy, and hardware mapping to each workload and target GPU.
  We present SparseDitto, an LLM-based system that constructs a GPU kernel for each matrix, operator, and target GPU. SparseDitto supports SpMV, SpMM, and SpGEMM within a unified design framework. A lightweight additive model ranks established strategies using structural features of the input matrix. An architecture-aware planner then proposes several candidate designs. Coding and verification agents implement and refine them using measurements from the target GPU. Across three sparse operators and a diverse set of matrices, SparseDitto achieves a geometric-mean speedup of 2.68x over cuSPARSE on an NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 GPU, with a maximum of 146.61x. On an NVIDIA H200 GPU, it achieves 2.79x, with a maximum of 78.5x. Its generated SpMM kernels also accelerate full-batch GCN training by up to 3.39x.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [105] [A Long-Run Persistence Theory for AI Systems under the Redundancy-Adjusted Artificial Age Score (AAS)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04012)
*Seyma Yaman Kayadibi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于AI系统长期持久性的理论框架，基于冗余调整的人工年龄得分（AAS），将该静态评估指标扩展为循环级泛函，并建立了包括有负担持久性、零负担持久性、振荡持久性和累积终末负担在内的渐近体制层级，核心定理表明AI系统可以在无限循环运行中保持结构年龄有界，而非不可避免地累积退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统越来越需要在重复的交互-适应-更新循环中运行，而非一次性输出。这引发了一个基础理论问题：AI系统能否无限期持续而不产生无界的结构性老化？现有的AAS仅是静态评估指标，无法刻画系统在多次运行周期中的长期演化行为。作者希望建立一个数学框架来形式化分析AI系统的长期持久性问题。

Method: 本文将AAS从静态评估度量扩展为循环级泛函，在每次循环中通过一个加权、冗余感知的对数罚函数来定义结构年龄。在此框架下证明循环级年龄良定义且一致有界，排除点态年龄爆炸。在此基础上定义了渐近体制的层级体系（有负担持久性、零负担持久性、振荡持久性、累积终末负担），并建立了多项数学性质包括：比较排序、敏感性界、分量逐步稳定下的收敛性、有限全变差下的持久性、阻尼循环间扰动下的几何稳定化，以及非退化冗余条件下的零负担刻画。

Result: 核心结果是：无限循环延续不需要无界的结构老化。AI系统可以经历无穷多个循环，同时其结构年龄保持有界；在更强的正则条件下，边际老化消失；在最强体制下，循环级负担收敛到零。文章还建立了多个数学定理涵盖收敛性、持久性和稳定性。

Conclusion: 该框架为分析AI系统的长期人工持久性提供了形式化基础，将其视为有界结构负担问题而非不可避免的累积恶化问题。这意味着AI持久性是可以被数学上保证的性质，而非无限退化的宿命。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence systems are increasingly expected to operate over repeated cycles of interaction, adaptation, and update rather than through isolated one-shot outputs. This raises a fundamental theoretical question: can an AI system persist indefinitely without incurring unbounded structural aging? This paper develops a long-run persistence framework for AI systems based on the redundancy-adjusted Artificial Age Score (AAS). The model extends AAS from a static evaluative measure into a cycle-level functional that generates an age sequence across repeated operation. At each cycle, structural age is defined through a weighted, redundancy-aware logarithmic penalty over component consistency levels. Within this framework, cycle-level age is shown to be well defined and uniformly bounded, thereby excluding explosive pointwise aging. On this basis, the paper defines a hierarchy of asymptotic regimes, including burdened persistence, zero-burden persistence, oscillatory persistence, and cumulative terminal burden. It also establishes comparative ordering, sensitivity bounds, convergence under componentwise stabilization, persistence under finite total variation, geometric stabilization under damped inter-cycle perturbations, and a zero-burden characterization under nondegenerate redundancy conditions. The main result is that indefinite cyclic continuation does not require unbounded structural aging: an AI system may pass through infinitely many cycles while its structural age remains bounded, while under stronger regularity conditions its marginal aging vanishes and, in the strongest regime, its cycle-level burden converges to zero. The framework thus provides a formal basis for analyzing long-run artificial persistence as a problem of bounded structural burden rather than inevitable cumulative deterioration.

</details>


### [106] [The LLM Proposes, the Executive Disposes: A Self-Verifying Agent Instrument that Dissociates Commitment Drift from Binding Drift in Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04066)
*Mohsen Arjmandi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结构性验证的智能体仪器：确定性执行机构掌管所有信念，语言模型只能提交类型化提案，主张需经事前注册的预测与代码观测匹配才能被采纳。该仪器具备自失效机制（打破写入错误、渲染大小或盐化金丝雀回显阈值即自判无效）和渲染不可见的影子参考系统，从而实现了对长时程智能体的结构性验证而非事后验证。实验表明，消融承诺机制使目标放弃率从0.00翻转至1.00，且所有运行均通过有效性门控；绑定通道因代码持有而结构性吸收了故障，仅在上游表现为假设形成的崩塌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体的自身状态和自我报告不可信，传统的事后验证方法无法有效验证其行为。作者旨在构建一种验证是结构性的而非事后性的智能体仪器，使得验证内嵌于系统设计本身。

Method: 仪器设计为三层结构：（1）确定性执行机构（Executive）持有全部信念；（2）语言模型仅能通过类型化提案（typed proposals）提交内容；（3）主张只有在行动前预注册的预测被代码观测匹配后才被接纳。关键机制包括：运行自失效机制（per-organ write-error、render-size、salted-canary-echo三类阈值）、渲染不可见的影子参考系统（在每一消融单元内编译完整系统本应提交的计划以定义漂移指标）、以及全披露的任务效能验证协议。

Result: 前八次架构运行中有四次因有效性门控失效而被判无效，且每次定位到真实缺陷。单变量消融实验（三种子每单元，最多394参考节拍每运行）表明：消融承诺机制使目标放弃率从0.00翻转为1.00，而绑定错误保持0.00平稳；绑定通道消融不表现为每节拍漂移，而是上游假设形成的崩塌。任务效能为零（52次门控运行在ARC-AGI-3上零级完成），作为前置注册的结构性否定条件披露。

Conclusion: 本工作提出了智能体开发的验证方法论及其使可测量的漂移分解框架。核心主张是：通过将验证结构化为系统内部机制而非外部事后审查，可以实现对不可信长时程智能体的可靠验证，并精确定位故障层级（机制层 vs. 代码层 vs. 上游认知层）。

Abstract: How do you verify a long-horizon agent when its own state and self-reports are exactly what you cannot trust? We present an agent instrument built so that verification is structural rather than post-hoc. A deterministic Executive owns all belief; a language model may only file typed proposals, and a claim is admitted only when a prediction pre-registered before acting is matched against observation by code. Two properties make the instrument a verifier of its own science, not just of the agent: every run invalidates itself when per-organ write-error, render-size, or salted-canary-echo floors are breached (four of the first eight architecture runs were invalidated, each localizing a real defect); and a render-invisible shadow reference compiles the plan the full system would have committed in every ablation cell, so drift metrics are defined even where the mechanism under test has been removed. Using this instrument we report a clean, single-variable result on a failure every long-horizon agent suffers: ablating the commitment mechanism flips goal-abandonment from 0.00 to 1.00 while binding error stays flat at 0.00 (three seeds per cell, up to 394 reference beats per run, every run gated valid). The binding channel, by contrast, does not reappear as per-beat drift when its repair is ablated -- because binding is code-owned, the failure class is structurally absorbed, its only residue appearing one layer upstream as a collapse in hypothesis formation. We report these under full disclosure that task efficacy is null (zero level completions across 52 gated runs on ARC-AGI-3), pre-registered as a structural defeater. The contribution is a verification methodology for agent development and the drift decomposition it makes measurable.

</details>


### [107] [Monte Carlo Tree Search for Table-to-Multimodal Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04071)
*Teng Lin,Zhiyang Zhang,Yuyu Luo,Nan Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MCTS-Report框架，利用蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）将多模态表格到报告的生成任务建模为结构化搜索空间中的渐进构建过程，通过原子动作分解和基于LLM的动态推理，实现了比现有方法更优的数值准确性、视觉质量和叙事连贯性，在自建基准MMRBench上取得77.9分的综合成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在固定线性流水线和子任务孤立处理的缺陷，无法在事实准确性、视觉质量和叙事连贯性之间进行联合优化。多模态报告自动生成需要在章节规划、可视化、洞察组织等环节间进行全局协调和自适应调整，而传统方法缺乏这种能力。

Method: 将报告生成分解为五种原子动作：章节规划、可视化任务识别、图表生成、洞察组织和叙事润色。每个动作由LLM基于当前报告状态进行动态推理执行。MCTS搜索过程中将推理轨迹存储在每个节点中以保证上下文一致。设计了多维奖励函数，联合评估数值事实一致性（通过SQL验证）、图表质量、图表-文本对齐和结构完整性，并引入多样性惩罚抑制重复图表和前置条件检查剪枝无效动作。同时构建了包含六个领域真实表格的MMRBench基准。

Result: 在MMRBench上的实验表明，MCTS-Report在结构完整性、数值准确性、图表-文本对齐和洞察新颖性方面显著优于强基线方法，综合得分达到77.9。

Conclusion: MCTS驱动的渐进式构建方式能够有效解决多模态报告生成中多目标联合优化难题，验证了将报告生成视为搜索问题的可行性，为该领域提供了新的研究方向和基准资源。

Abstract: Automatically generating professional multimodal reports comprising both textual analysis and visual charts from structured tabular data is a critical challenge in data intelligence. Existing methods suffer from fixed linear pipelines and isolated subtask processing, which hinder joint optimization of factual accuracy, visual quality, and narrative coherence. To address these issues, this paper proposes MCTS-Report, a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS)-driven framework that formulates multimodal table-to-report generation as a progressive construction process over a structured search space. The core idea is to decompose report generation into atomic actions, including chapter planning, visualization task identification, chart generation, insight organization, and narrative refinement, each executed by an LLM based on dynamic reasoning conditioned on the current report state. We use an LLM to generate step-by-step reasoning and actions during MCTS, storing the reasoning trajectory in each node for context-aware, coherent report construction. To guide the search, we design a multi-dimensional reward function that jointly evaluates numerical fact consistency (via SQL), chart quality, chart-text alignment, and structural completeness, while incorporating a diversity penalty to suppress repeated charts and a precondition check to prune invalid actions. We also construct MMRBench, a comprehensive benchmark comprising real-world tables from six domains, paired with expert-refined reference report structures and verifiable key insights. Experiments on MMRBench demonstrate that MCTS-Report significantly outperforms strong baselines across structural completeness, numerical accuracy, chart-text alignment, and insight novelty, achieving a 77.9 overall score.

</details>


### [108] [FinProBench: Evaluating Financial AI Agents with Role-Grounded Rubrics Derived from Professional Deliverables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04077)
*Ben Wang,Kang Zhou,Lifan Guo,Feng Chen,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 FinProBench 基准和 Role-Grounded Rubric Construction (RGRC) 管道，用于从从业者交付物中提取评分标准以评估金融 AI Agent。RGRC 在角色专门化任务上显著优于基于提示词的方法（99.1% vs 78.0%），并可将评分标准构建成本降低 6.7 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融 AI Agent 评估的评分标准方法通常从任务提示或模型输出中推导，忽略了从业者交付物中隐含的隐性专业标准。这导致评估标准与真实专业工作脱节，特别是对于模型先验中缺乏的专门化角色任务。

Method: 引入 RGRC 管道，包含四个阶段：交付物收集、能力提取、评分标准综合和验证。将 57 个职业按交付物类型分类为 30 个先验丰富的常规角色和 27 个先验稀少的专门化角色。构建 FinProBench，包含 1,723 个精选交付物，覆盖 57 个职业、8 个金融子行业和 161 种交付物类型，并发布包含 20 个完整任务的初始评估集。

Result: 对于常规角色，Prompt-only 与 RGRC 表现接近（89.2% vs 90.7%）；对于角色专门化任务，RGRC 大幅超越（99.1% vs 78.0%）。在异质 LLM 评委和角色级评分标准下，人工交付物平均排名第一（73.7 vs 70.3, 70.2, 69.6），四个系统置信区间重叠且各有互补优势。角色级评分标准复用使每次任务构建成本降低 6.7 倍。

Conclusion: 提示工程在模型先验充分表示惯例时可近似评分标准，但超出先验的标准需要专业级基础。RGRC 通过从业者交付物捕获隐性标准，提供可迁移的评估方案，且显著降低评分标准构建成本，为金融 AI Agent 评估提供了更贴近真实专业工作的方法论。

Abstract: Evaluating financial AI agents requires criteria aligned with real professional work. Existing rubric methods typically derive criteria from task prompts or model outputs, overlooking tacit standards visible only in practitioner deliverables. We introduce FinProBench, a benchmark for professional financial tasks, and Role-Grounded Rubric Construction (RGRC), a reusable pipeline that derives rubrics from deliverables produced by practitioners in the same role. RGRC comprises four stages: Deliverable Collection, Competency Extraction, Rubric Synthesis, and Validation. Its rubrics capture tacit standards, distinguish quality levels, and transfer across tasks within a role. Before analysis, we classified 57 occupations by deliverable genre into 30 prior-rich conventional roles and 27 prior-sparse role-specialized roles. Across all roles, Prompt-only nearly matches RGRC for conventional roles (89.2% vs. 90.7%), but RGRC substantially outperforms it for role-specialized roles (99.1% vs. 78.0%). This split indicates that prompt engineering can approximate rubrics when conventions are well represented in model priors, while professional grounding is essential for standards beyond those priors. FinProBench is built from 1,723 curated deliverables spanning 57 occupations, 8 financial sub-industries, and 161 deliverable types, and releases an initial evaluation set of 20 complete tasks covering 20 roles in 7 sub-industries. With heterogeneous LLM judges and role-level rubrics, human deliverables rank first on average (73.7 vs. 70.3, 70.2, and 69.6 out of 100), while all four systems show overlapping 95% confidence intervals and complementary strengths. Reusing rubrics at the role level reduces estimated per-task construction effort by 6.7 times relative to authoring each rubric from scratch.

</details>


### [109] [FinPerMA: A Theory-Informed, Event-Grounded Personalized-Memory Benchmark for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04095)
*Ben Wang,Kang Zhou,Lifan Guo,Feng Chen,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FinPerMA，一个基于事件的个性化记忆基准，用于评估LLM智能体在长期纵向投资者轨迹中的记忆保持与偏好适应能力。实验表明，7个前沿LLM和多种记忆配置在所有上下文配置下均远未饱和（总体准确率约0.47），且基于摘要的记忆常丢失偏好信号，简单检索反而优于专用记忆系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的个性化记忆基准主要测试事实保留或依赖约束较弱的模型生成轨迹，缺乏对事件驱动的偏好适应能力的评估。在金融建议等高风险场景中，LLM智能体能否在长时间跨度内维护和更新个性化用户模型仍不清楚，因此需要更严谨的事件驱动基准来填补这一空白。

Method: FinPerMA的生成流程结合了三种要素：(1) 确定性的、基于理论的冲击影响规则；(2) 受控的LLM叙述生成；(3) 自动化质量筛查。基准基于冻结的纵向投资者轨迹构建，包含Post-Shock检查点以隔离智能体是否将重大事件整合到其持久用户模型中。数据集包含2,994个问题，覆盖276个用户画像。

Result: 在2,994个问题上测试了7个前沿LLM和最多7种记忆配置，结果远未饱和：没有全上下文配置超过约0.47的总体准确率或多选题约39%的准确率。归因分析显示，基于摘要的记忆常保留事实细节但丢失个性化所需的偏好信号，简单检索在冲击事件后与专用记忆系统的性能差距进一步拉大。

Conclusion: 当前LLM智能体在事件驱动的个性化记忆方面仍存在显著不足，尤其在偏好信号捕捉和冲击后适应方面。该发现为建模个性化记忆系统提供了重要启示：简单的检索策略可能比复杂的专用记忆系统更有效，而基于摘要的记忆需要在保留事实与偏好信号之间取得更好平衡。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly used as personalized assistants in high-stakes domains such as financial advising, yet it remains unclear whether they can maintain and update an individualized user model over long horizons. Existing personalized-memory benchmarks primarily test factual retention or rely on weakly constrained model-generated trajectories, leaving event-driven preference adaptation underexplored. We introduce FinPerMA, an event-grounded benchmark that evaluates personalized memory against frozen longitudinal investor trajectories. Its generation pipeline combines deterministic, theory-informed impact rules, controlled LLM narration, and automated quality screening; a Post-Shock checkpoint isolates whether an agent has integrated a material event into its persistent user model. On 2,994 questions from 276 personas, seven frontier LLMs and up to seven memory configurations remain far from saturated: no full-context configuration exceeds approximately 0.47 overall accuracy or approximately 39% on multiple-choice questions. Attribution analysis shows that summary-based memory often preserves factual details while losing the preference signals needed for personalization; simple retrieval can therefore outperform purpose-built memory systems, with the gap widening after shocks.

</details>


### [110] [BrainBench: Benchmarking Large Language Models for Comprehensive EEG Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04156)
*Yangxuan Zhou,Sha Zhao,Yuning Chen,Chen Wu,Jiquan Wang,Shijian Li,Gang Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为综合脑电图理解基准的统一基准，用于评估大语言模型在指令条件下的全面EEG理解能力，涵盖17个数据集、多个任务和超过10万次执行，通过两种范式（CodeAct自主代码执行和BrainAgent结构化智能体分析）对多类代表性LLM进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的EEG分析评估主要聚焦于孤立的解码任务或系统特定的演示，未能量化LLM在"综合EEG理解"（即连接自然语言指令、信号处理、定量证据和科学解释的完整工作流）方面的能力。需要一个统一的基准来标准化评估LLM在全面EEG理解上的表现。

Method: 构建统一基准，包含四个子集（基础分析、睡眠评估、神经认知评估和生理整合），覆盖17个数据集、多种任务和大量真实数据实例。系统需根据指令和EEG记录（可选生理信号）进行分析并生成科学报告，输出通过数值、分类、集合、序列、语义和工件等多维度验证。在CodeAct（自主代码执行）和BrainAgent（结构化智能体分析）两种执行范式下对多类代表性LLM进行超过10万次评估。

Result: 不同模型在子集、难度级别和执行范式上的表现差异显著，表明EEG能力不仅取决于模型本身，还取决于其操作化方式。基准提供了可复现的测试平台用于推进基于LLM的EEG理解，代码和基准即将发布，评估结果将持续更新。

Conclusion: 该基准为量化LLM在综合EEG理解上的能力提供了标准化的可复现测试平台，揭示了模型选择与执行范式对EEG分析能力的重要影响，为未来推进LLM在神经科学领域的应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) analysis extends beyond assigning predefined labels to recordings; it requires workflows connecting natural-language instructions, signal processing, quantitative evidence, and scientific interpretation. We term this capability \emph{comprehensive EEG understanding}. Existing evaluations, however, primarily target isolated decoding tasks or system-specific demonstrations, leaving the competence of large language models (LLMs) insufficiently quantified. We introduce \benchmarkname{}, a unified benchmark for comprehensive, instruction-conditioned EEG understanding. It comprises four subsets---Foundational Analysis, Sleep Assessment, Neurocognitive Assessment, and Physiological Integration---covering 17 datasets, \numcases{} tasks, and over \numinstances{} real-data instances. Given an instruction and EEG recordings with optional physiological signals, a system must perform the analysis and produce a scientifically grounded report and, when required, artifacts. Outputs are assessed through numerical, categorical, set, sequence, semantic, and artifact validation. We evaluate \nummodels{} representative LLMs across more than 100K executions under two paradigms: autonomous code execution with CodeAct and structured agentic analysis with BrainAgent. Results vary substantially across models, subsets, difficulty levels, and execution paradigms, showing that EEG competence depends on the model and its operationalization. \benchmarkname{} provides a reproducible testbed for advancing LLM-based EEG understanding. The code and benchmark will be released soon, with evaluation results continuously updated.

</details>


### [111] [Adversarially Robust Abductive Fusion of Pre-trained Transformer-based Perception Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04190)
*Mario Leiva,Yue Ma,Qinru Qiu,Gerardo Simari,Paulo Shakarian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需领域知识的元认知层用于融合多个预训练视觉检测器。通过从每个模型自身训练嵌入构建标签向量池(LVP)，利用向量空间的几何特性推导误差检测规则，与基于领域知识的规则性能相当(测试集F1差异在0.002以内)。整体框架将多检测器融合建模为基于一致性的溯因问题，在测试时通过整数规划和多项式时间启发式求解。在15个天气偏移测试集和6个ViT检测器上验证，该无领域知识方法在干净数据上与最强多数投票变体相当(F1差异0.005以内)，且在90%标签翻转攻击下平均F1为0.42对比0.35，相对提升22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署预训练感知模型于新环境时，分布偏移会显著降低其精度，且简单的模型集成(如多数投票)无法恢复精度——它们以召回率换取精确率，且对协调性失败很脆弱。先前的元认知方法通过学习逻辑规则来标记模型错误，但依赖手工编写的领域知识线索(如物体尺寸先验、分割掩码)，这些线索无法迁移到真正新颖的场景。因此需要一种无需领域知识即可学习的元认知层。

Method: (1) 利用向量空间几何构建每模型的标签向量池(LVP)，基于训练嵌入构建原型，从检测结果与训练原型之间的几何关系推导误差检测规则；(2) 保持神经符号方法，几何规则与领域知识规则共享同一逻辑框架，可在有条件时互补；(3) 将多ViT检测器的融合建模为基于一致性的溯因问题，在测试时通过精确整数规划(IP)和多项式时间启发式求解。

Result: 在航空影像基准(15个天气偏移测试集、6个ViT检测器)上：(1) 无领域知识的元认知层在干净数据上与最强多数投票变体性能相当(F1差异在0.005以内)；(2) 在协调标签翻转攻击下保持性能，90%翻转率时平均F1为0.42对比多数投票0.35，相对提升22%；(3) 翻转率超过0.4后，在所有测试集上均取得最高F1。

Conclusion: 元认知误检检测层可以不依赖任何领域知识，仅通过向量空间的几何结构(检测结果与训练原型之间的关系)学习，达到与领域知识规则相当的精度。该方法保持神经符号特性，既保留了逻辑框架的可解释性和可扩展性，又避免了人工领域知识的迁移局限，且在对抗性(协调性失败)场景下表现出显著的鲁棒性优势。

Abstract: Deploying pre-trained perception models in novel environments degrades their accuracy under distributional shift, and assembling them alone does not recover it: combiners such as majority voting trade recall for precision and are brittle to coordinated failures. Prior metacognitive methods learn logical rules that flag a model's errors, but rely on hand-authored domain-knowledge cues (object-size priors, segmentation masks) that do not transfer to genuinely novel scenes. We show that this metacognitive layer can be learned without any domain knowledge by exploiting vector-space geometry: per-model Label Vector Pools (LVP), built from each model's own training embeddings, yield error-detection rules from the geometry of detections relative to training-determined prototypes, reaching parity with domain-knowledge rules to within $0.002$ every F1 on test set. Because the approach remains neurosymbolic, these geometric rules share a single logical framework and can still be complemented by domain knowledge when available. We frame the fusion of multiple imperfect ViT-based detectors as a consistency-based abduction problem solved at test time by an exact Integer Program (IP) and a polynomial-time heuristic. On an aerial-imagery benchmark of 15 weather-shifted test sets and six ViT detectors, our domain-knowledge-free layer matches the strongest majority-vote variant on clean data (within $0.005$ F1) and, unlike every majority-vote baseline, retains its performance under a coordinated label-flipping attack: at a $90\%$ flip rate it averages $0.42$ F1 versus $0.35$ for MV-Plurality (a $22\%$ relative gain) and attains the highest F1 on \emph{every} test set once the flip rate exceeds $0.4$

</details>


### [112] [MatrAIx: Simulating the World with 8.3 Billion Persona Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04205)
*Xiaomin Li,Yuexing Hao,Jianheng Hou,Jintao Huang,Qianfeng Wen,Shirley Huang,Yifan Liu,Xiaoyi Liu,Yilan Fan,Yijun Wang,Koutian Wu,Ruoqi Gao,Muhammad Ahmed Mohsin,Jing Tang,Brihi Joshi,Heming Liu,Zheyuan Deng,Zonglin Di,Sankalp Jajee,Jiuyao Lu,Zhiwei Zhang,Saksham Kapoor,Ishan Gupta,Yunhan Zhao,Chanwoo Park,Yucheng Lu,Bing Hu,Weihang Xiao,Aravind Mohan,Hanwen Xing,Runyu Zhang,Mihir Kulshreshtha,Yuanda Xu,Qianyu Zhu,Dianzhuo Wang,Yuxin Xiao,Bowen Jiang,Yongye Su,Wenhao Chai,Zuxin Liu,Lawrence Yunliang Chen,Xuandong Zhao,Ethan Ye,Shivam Patel,Jason Xie,Alex Martin Richmond,Weixiang Ding,Emre Okcular,Diya Mathew,Ziheng Wang,Rana M. Shahroz Khan,Zhejian Peng,Fang Wu,Fan Nie,Xinyang Han,Yubin Kim,Jiawei Zhang,Zhenting Qi,Huangyuan Su,Xu Pan,Abinitha Gourabathina,Hyewon Jeong,Hemanth Neelgund Ramesh,Kumail Alhamoud,Kimia Hamidieh,Zidi Xiong,Samuel Schmidgall,Pengrui Han,Yepeng Huang,Yongheng Wang,Bowen Yang,Alex Gu,Yuchu Wang,Akshay Paruchuri,Brenna Li,Hejie Cui,Jiayuan Ding,Chaosheng Dong,Jiahao Wang,Yixuan He,Chi Wang,Pamela Bhattacharya,Tianyi Peng,Paul Pu Liang,Mitchell Gordon,Yilun Du,Marinka Zitnik,James Zou,Prasanna Tambe,Philip Torr,Emily Fox,Asu Ozdaglar,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MatrAIx，一个面向 AI 系统和数字产品的大规模模拟用户评测基础设施，包含 83 亿条画像记录（Persona 8B）、四种交互环境（Survey、AI Chatbot、Web、App）以及 1010 个跨 25+ 领域的应用任务，通过 18,189 次评估试验验证了其在多样化用户画像下的行为模拟能力，画像遵循率达 91.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类对 AI 系统和数字产品进行评测成本高昂、速度慢且难以扩展；离线评测虽可扩展但往往忽略人类多样性和交互行为。因此需要一种既能规模化又能模拟异构用户行为的评测基础设施。

Method: MatrAIx 由三个核心组件构成：(1) Persona 8B，包含 83 亿条画像记录，由 1,290 个分类维度表示，记录从保留相关属性的依赖图中采样或来源于人工撰写的画像，并发布约 100 万条质量过滤后的核心数据集（599,847 条人类基元 + 400,000 条合成数据）；(2) MatrAIx Playground 提供四种交互环境（调查问卷、AI 聊天机器人、Web、App）；(3) 提供 1,010 个应用任务，覆盖商业、软件、金融、医疗等 25+ 领域。实验使用三个 LLM（Claude Opus 4.8、GPT 5.5、Claude Haiku 4.5）驱动画像 Agent，在八个代表性任务上开展了 18,189 次评估试验。

Result: 评测反馈捕捉了不同画像背景下的决策与偏好差异，如价格上涨后的犹豫、AI 助手失败后的继续意愿以及延迟容忍度。两项验证研究：(1) 400 次受控试验中，画像遵循性在十个行为属性和四种环境中表现正确（表达或正确抑制）的比率为 91.5%；(2) 人类与 LLM 评审员评估了人类基元画像的提取质量。

Conclusion: MatrAIx 提供了一个端到端的基础设施，可用多样化模拟用户对 AI 系统和数字产品进行规模化评测，弥补了传统人工评测与离线评测之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Human evaluation of AI systems and digital products is costly, slow, and difficult to scale. Offline evaluations are more scalable but often abstract away human diversity and interactive behavior. We therefore introduce MatrAIx, a population-scale simulated-user evaluation infrastructure for testing AI systems and digital products with heterogeneous users. MatrAIx has three core components: First, Persona 8B contains 8.3 billion persona records represented by 1,290 categorical dimensions. Records are either sampled from a dependency graph that preserves correlated attributes or derived from human-authored profiles. We release a quality-filtered coreset of approximately 1 million personas, comprising 599,847 human-grounded and 400,000 synthetic records. Second, the MatrAIx Playground provides four environments in which diverse users evaluate and interact with digital products: Survey, AI Chatbot, Web, and App. Third, MatrAIx provides 1,010 application tasks spanning more than 25 domains, including Commerce, Software, Finance, and Healthcare. We conducted 18,189 evaluation trials across eight representative tasks. Persona agents were powered by three LLMs: Claude Opus 4.8, GPT 5.5, and Claude Haiku 4.5. The resulting feedback captures how decisions and preferences vary across persona backgrounds, including hesitation after a price increase, willingness to continue after an AI assistant fails, and latency tolerance. We conducted two main validation studies: First, a 400-trial controlled study evaluated persona adherence across ten behavioral attributes and all four environments. The declared behavior was expressed or correctly suppressed in 366 trials (91.5%). Second, human and LLM judges evaluated the extraction quality of human-grounded personas. Overall, MatrAIx provides an end-to-end infrastructure for evaluating AI systems and digital products with diverse simulated human users.

</details>


### [113] [Interoceptive Attention as Dynamic Homeostatic Prioritization in a Foraging Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04232)
*St John Grimbly,Nicolas Kuske,Evert A. Boonstra,Bruce A. Bassett,Charel van Hoof,Rowan Hodson,Benjamin Rosman,Ryan Smith,Mark Solms,Jonathan P. Shock*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究有限感知带宽下生物系统如何分配感知精度。作者在基于主动推理的四通道觅食网格世界（AffectWorld）中，提出一种选择性内感受精度分配机制：代理人在每一步读取自身身体状态信念，识别最需要的通道，将固定预算的内感受精度重新分配给该通道。相比均匀精度分配，该方法在匹配预算下使学习阶段的存活率翻倍以上（0.414 vs 0.199，p≤10⁻⁴）。研究发现收益同时来自规划与感知，且必须与需求对齐（指向最不需要的通道反而比均匀分配更差），被关注的通道动力学学习速度约快两倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物系统必须在有限感知带宽下调节相互竞争的需求，提升某一估计的精度会牺牲其他估计的精度。因此任何固定预算的系统都必须决定在哪里分配感知精度。作者希望研究这一精度分配问题在被迫满足多种身体需求的觅食智能体中的表现。

Method: 采用主动推理框架构建觅食代理。代理在四通道网格世界AffectWorld中，每一步读取自身身体状态信念，识别最紧迫需求的通道，并将固定预算的内感受精度重新分配给该通道。同等精度形状的似然函数同时用于信念更新与规划。通过与均匀精度分配代理对比，在11种布局、各32个随机种子上进行配对聚类bootstrap检验。

Result: 选择性精度分配使学习阶段存活率提升至均匀精度代理的两倍以上（0.414 vs 0.199，p≤10⁻⁴）。该收益部分源于规划（若仅对感知器提供该似然而不给规划器，收益约减半）；收益与需求对齐（瞄准最不需要的通道表现差于均匀分配）；被关注的通道其动力学学习速度约快两倍，即使在匹配观测次数下仍保持领先。

Conclusion: 有限预算下的感知精度分配应具有需求选择性：将精度投向最需要的通道能显著提升生存表现，且该机制同时经由感知与规划两条路径起作用。这种精度路由不仅影响生存，还会在学习速度上留下可观测的行为痕迹。研究为理解生物体如何在有限感知资源下协调多重内稳态需求提供了计算框架。

Abstract: Biological systems must regulate competing needs under limited perceptual bandwidth, where sharpening one estimate costs the capacity to sharpen the others. Any fixed-budget system therefore has to decide where to allocate its perceptual precision. We study this in a foraging agent that must keep several bodily needs satisfied to survive, modelled with active inference. At each step it reads its own body-state beliefs, identifies the most-needed channel, and reallocates a fixed budget of interoceptive precision toward it, so that the same precision-shaped likelihood feeds both belief update and planning. In AffectWorld, a four-channel foraging gridworld, this selective allocation more than doubles learning-phase survival at matched budget against a uniform-precision agent ($0.414$ vs $0.199$ across 11 layouts, $n{=}32$ seeds each, paired cluster-bootstrap $p \leq 10^{-4}$). Two further results sharpen the mechanism. The benefit runs through planning as well as perception, since denying the shaped likelihood to the planner alone removes about half of it. It is also need-aligned, since aiming precision at the least-needed channel does worse than spreading it evenly. The attended channel additionally learns its own dynamics about twice as fast, and stays ahead even at matched observation count, a behavioural trace of the same precision routing, visible in learning speed, not survival.

</details>


### [114] [The RAIL Principles for Neurosymbolic AI: Reasoning, Assurances, Interfacing and Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04285)
*Agnese Chiatti,Michael Cochez,Cristina Cornelio,Sebastijan Dumancic,Artur d'Avila Garcez,Luis C. Lamb,Lia Morra,Mathias Niepert,Robert Peharz,Alberto Speranzon,Maarten Stol,Annette Ten Teije,Thiviyan Thanapalasingam,Frank Van Harmelen,Emile Van Krieken,Antonio Vergari,Benjie Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出神经符号AI设计的四大原则（RAIL：推理Reasoning、保证Assurances、接口Interfacing、学习Learning），主张机器学习与形式推理的结合并非小众方法，而是构建可靠、高效、可信AI系统的关键路径。通过RAIL框架可统一分析各种主流AI系统（从物理感知机器学习到Alpha系列、因果学习和工具增强的大语言模型），为工程师在AI系统设计与部署中提供更有原则性的决策指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经符号AI正快速受到关注，但被视为一种小众方法。作者认为这种观点有误——机器学习与形式推理的结合对开发可靠、高效且值得信赖的AI系统至关重要，需要一种统一视角重新审视当前AI系统的设计。

Method: 作者提出了神经符号AI设计的四大原则——RAIL框架：1) Reasoning（推理）：符号推理与机器学习互补；2) Assurances（保证）：提供可验证的可靠性保障；3) Interfacing（接口）：连接神经与符号组件；4) Learning（学习）：从数据中学习以增强符号组件。文中通过多个AI领域（物理感知机器学习、神经引导搜索如Alpha系列、因果学习、工具增强大语言模型）的系统分析验证该框架的有效性。

Result: RAIL框架为看似不相关的AI系统提供了统一的分析视角，表明许多传统上不被视为神经符号的领先AI系统实际上都符合神经符号设计原则。该框架能使工程师在设计和部署生产级AI系统时做出更明智、更有原则的决策。

Conclusion: 神经符号方法应被视为AI主流设计范式而非边缘方法。RAIL原则作为实践指南，将指导工程技术人员将神经符号方法整合到下一代AI技术中，从而推动可靠、高效且可信的AI系统的发展。

Abstract: Neurosymbolic AI systems that integrate machine learning and symbolic reasoning are rapidly gaining attention. They complement the data-intensive statistical approaches of neural networks and language models with symbolic reasoning algorithms to function in high-stakes domains or in low-data regimes that characterize many real-world applications. We argue that the neurosymbolic combination of machine learning and formal reasoning is not a niche approach within AI, but rather includes many already successful techniques that are of crucial importance to the development of reliable, efficient and, ultimately, trustworthy systems. This perspective prompts a re-examination of the design of current AI systems. We show that many leading AI systems, including some that are not traditionally considered as neurosymbolic, can be analysed from the perspective of four principles of neurosymbolic AI design: Reasoning, Assurances, Interfacing and Learning (RAIL). Applying the RAIL framework offers a unified view of seemingly disparate AI systems, ranging from physics-aware machine learning to neuro-guided search (such as Google DeepMind's Alpha-* suite), causal learning and tool-augmented Large Language Models. Importantly, the RAIL principles will enable engineers to make better-informed and more principled decisions about the design and deployment of production-level AI systems. In this article, we introduce the RAIL principles, examine how they can be applied across major areas of AI, and illustrate how they may guide practitioners to integrate neurosymbolic methods into next-generation AI technologies.

</details>


### [115] [SafeCommit: Certifying When Memory-Grounded Agents May Safely Act](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04289)
*Mayur Akewar,Ravi Ranjan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SafeCommit，一种位于代理推理与外部执行之间的风险受控层，用于解决长时程代理在记忆不确定性下的过早承诺问题。该层通过构建校准的潜在世界集合，仅当conformal action certificate证明动作在所有保留世界中安全时才允许执行，否则选择低副作用探针或保守回退。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程代理依赖持久记忆和工具执行具有外部副作用的动作，但记忆可能过时、冲突、不完整或损坏，导致代理在不确证记忆基础的情况下过早采取行动。这是一个核心失败模式，需要形式化解决安全承诺问题。

Method: SafeCommit在代理推理与外部执行之间插入一个风险受控层。该层从记忆、观察、工具输出、来源信息和策略约束构建校准的潜在世界集合（plausible latent worlds）。只有当conformal action certificate证明动作在所有保留世界中均安全时才允许执行；否则选择针对阻塞认证世界的低副作用探针，或返回保守回退。在日历世界覆盖率下，不安全认证承诺的概率有界于目标水平α，且对不完美的世界提议，界将校准误差与表示误差分离。

Result: 通过一个无依赖的可控模拟器展示了安全性-效用权衡，并可用一条命令复现所有报告结果。安全性保证包括：在日历世界覆盖下，不安全认证承诺概率不超过目标水平α；即使世界提议不完美，界限也能将校准误差和表示误差分离。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个具体方法，不仅决定代理应该做什么，还决定可用证据何时足以安全地执行该动作，从而解决长时程代理在记忆不确定性下的安全承诺问题。

Abstract: Long-horizon agents increasingly use persistent memory and tools to take actions with external side effects. A central failure mode is premature commitment: an agent acts before resolving whether its memory grounding is stale, conflicting, incomplete, or corrupted. We formalize this problem as safe commitment under memory uncertainty and introduce SafeCommit, a risk controlled layer between agent reasoning and external execution. The layer constructs a calibrated set of plausible latent worlds from memory, observations, tool outputs, provenance, and policy constraints. It permits a side effectful action only when a conformal action certificate shows that the action is safe in every retained world. Otherwise, it selects a low-side-effect probe that targets the worlds blocking certification, or returns a conservative fallback. Under calibrated world coverage, the probability of an unsafe certified commit is at most the target level α; with imperfect world proposal, the bound separates calibration and representation error. A dependency-free controlled simulator illustrates the safety-utility tradeoff and reproduces all reported results with one command. The goal is to offer a concrete approach for deciding not only what an agent should do, but when the available evidence is sufficient to safely do it.

</details>


### [116] [NeuMoSync: End-to-End Neuromodulatory Control for Plasticity and Adaptability in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04358)
*Seyed Roozbeh Razavi Rohani,Khashayar Khajavi,Wesley Chung,Mandana Samiei,Mo Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NeuMoSync架构，通过为每个神经元引入可学习的特征向量和更高抽象层次的调制模块，模拟大脑的全局神经调节机制，动态调节激活动态和突触可塑性，以解决持续学习中深度网络的塑性损失和知识迁移不足问题。在多个CL基准上验证了其保持塑性和改善前后向迁移的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习要求模型顺序学习多个任务，但深度神经网络在持续学习过程中常常遭受塑性损失（plasticity loss）和知识迁移能力不足的问题，这限制了模型的长期适应性。作者从大脑的全局神经调节机制中获得启发，希望将这种全局协调机制引入深度学习系统，以增强模型的适应性和可塑性。

Method: NeuMoSync架构包含两个核心组件：(1) 为每个神经元引入可学习的特征向量，用于追踪网络范围内的历史上下文；(2) 一个在更高抽象层次运行的调制模块，该模块基于当前输入和网络演化状态合成神经元特定的调制信号，自适应地调节神经元的激活动态和突触可塑性。

Result: 在多种CL基准上评估，包括随机标签CIFAR-10/MNIST（记忆任务）、Shuffle CIFAR-10/Mini-ImageNet（概念漂移）、Class Split ImageNet/CIFAR-100（类增量学习）和Permuted MNIST（域增量学习），NeuMoSync在保持塑性方面表现优异，并在前向和后向适应上相比现有方法均有提升。消融实验验证了各组件的必要性，调制信号分析揭示了可解释的跨任务协调模式。

Conclusion: 将全局协调机制（神经调节）融入深度学习系统是提升持续学习鲁棒性和适应性的有效途径，NeuMoSync为构建更稳健的自适应持续学习系统提供了新的思路，代码已开源。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) requires models to learn tasks sequentially, yet deep neural networks often suffer from plasticity loss and poor knowledge transfer, which can impede their long-term adaptability. Drawing high-level inspiration from global neuromodulatory mechanisms in the brain, we introduce Neuromodulation and Synchronization (NeuMoSync), a novel architecture that integrates dynamic, neuron-specific modulation into deep neural networks to enhance their adaptability and plasticity. NeuMoSync extends standard neural network architectures with learnable feature vectors for each neuron that track network-wide historical context and with a module operating at a higher level of abstraction. This module synthesizes neuron-specific signals, conditioned on both current inputs and the network's evolving state, to adaptively regulate activation dynamics and synaptic plasticity. Evaluated on diverse CL benchmarks, including memorization (Random Label CIFAR-10 and Random Label MNIST), concept drift (Shuffle CIFAR-10 and Shuffle Mini-ImageNet), class-incremental learning (Class Split ImageNet and Class Split CIFAR-100), and domain-incremental learning (Permuted MNIST), NeuMoSync demonstrates strong performance in retaining plasticity and achieves improvements in both forward and backward adaptation compared with existing methods. Ablation studies validate the necessity of each component, while analysis of the learned modulatory signals reveals interpretable coordination patterns across tasks. Our work underscores the potential of integrating global coordination mechanisms into deep learning systems to advance robust, adaptive continual learning. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/RoozbehRazavi/NeuMoSync.

</details>


### [117] [Improving Auto-Design of Neural PDE Solvers with a Domain-Specific Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04384)
*Shengxin Kong,Liwen Xu,Jingwen Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ADSL-PDE提出了一种结构化搜索状态表示方法，将神经PDE求解器的自动设计从无限制Python代码空间重塑为功能性设计决策空间，通过确定性编译器将有效搜索状态映射为可执行求解器，使进化智能体能够在设计决策层面进行高效的搜索与优化，多个PDE基准上在前十次进化迭代内实现了超过52%的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无限制Python代码空间中，有效的PDE求解器构成极其稀疏的子集，大多数候选程序存在语法错误、语义不兼容或数值不稳定问题，导致LLM直接进行代码生成时大部分搜索能力浪费在实现失败上而无法聚焦于求解器质量本身。

Method: ADSL-PDE引入概念与可执行代码之间的结构化搜索状态，该状态表示决定神经PDE求解器功能的关键决策（架构、物理约束、目标函数、采样和优化），同时抽象掉底层实现细节；通过确定性编译器将每个有效搜索状态映射为可执行求解器，并在此表示之上构建进化智能体，迭代地提出、评估和精炼求解器搜索状态。

Result: 在多个PDE基准上，ADSL-PDE同时提升了搜索效率和优化稳定性，在前十次进化迭代内实现了超过52%的改进。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的自动设计智能体不仅需要更强的推理能力，更需要一种能够将探索集中于有效且关键决策的搜索表示；这一原则可能适用于更广泛的自动设计场景。

Abstract: Neural PDE solver auto-design is fundamentally a search-space representation problem. In the space of unrestricted Python programs, valid solvers form an extremely sparse subset: most candidate programs are syntactically incorrect, semantically incompatible, or numerically unstable. Direct code generation therefore forces an LLM to spend most of its search capacity navigating implementation failures rather than reasoning about solver quality. ADSL-PDE addresses this challenge by introducing a structured search state between solver concepts and executable code. It represents the functional decisions that determine a neural PDE solver (architecture, physical constraints, objectives, sampling, and optimization) while abstracting away low-level implementation details. A deterministic compiler maps each valid search state to an executable solver. In effect, ADSL-PDE reshapes the search space: it removes large regions of invalid programs, increases the density of meaningful candidates, and preserves the compositional freedom needed to discover previously unseen designs. Solver evolution can thus operate over design decisions rather than code artifacts. Built on this representation, our evolutionary agent iteratively proposes, evaluates, and refines solver search states using empirical feedback. Across multiple PDE benchmarks, ADSL-PDE improves both search efficiency and optimization stability, achieving an improvement of more than 52% within the first ten evolution iterations. These results suggest a broader principle for LLM-driven auto-design: effective agents do not merely require stronger reasoning, but rather a search representation that concentrates exploration on valid and consequential decisions.

</details>


### [118] [Architectural Implications of Agentic AI Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04458)
*Jirong Yang,Peizhe Liu,Chaojie Zhang,Jovan Stojkovic*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次对数据中心中的Agentic AI工作负载进行架构特征化研究，基于微软Azure的生产数据和开源框架的受控实验，揭示了代理工作流的碎片化、异构性和突发性特征，并提出Agora原型系统来优化商品服务器的资源利用率和代理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI正在数据中心兴起，但其对服务器架构的影响尚未被探索。传统的统一服务器设计可能与代理工作负载的特性存在严重不匹配，因此需要系统性地理解其架构特征，以指导未来服务器设计。

Method: 首先通过分类法组织代理工作流，结合微软Azure生产环境的实测数据和开源框架的受控实验进行架构特征化；基于发现设计Agora原型系统，包括动态回收空闲CPU核进行共置吞吐作业、GPU内存超订与状态预取/换页隐藏开销、按角色划分CPU核池并采用亲和性感知调度恢复局部性，以及自动调优机制。

Result: 代理执行呈现碎片化与异构性：请求扩展为反复跨越CPU-GPU边界的工作流（LLM推理、工具调用、编排决策）。CPU处于关键路径，负载随时间保持低位但伴随突发尖峰；模型组合决定GPU使用的均匀性；任务和工具的多样性进一步扩大负载范围。Agora在提升利用率和服务器吞吐量的同时保持代理尾延迟。

Conclusion: 传统统一服务器架构与代理工作负载存在三方面错配：碎片化执行导致CPU和GPU容量被闲置、同质化CPU供应难以匹配多样化的软件角色、多代理共享核心损害微架构局部性。未来需要面向Agentic AI设计异构、自适应、延迟感知的服务器架构。

Abstract: Agentic AI is emerging in datacenters, but its architectural implications remain unexplored. We organize agentic workflows in a taxonomy and present its first architectural characterization with a production study at Microsoft Azure and a controlled study of open-source frameworks. We show that agentic execution is fragmented and heterogeneous. Requests expand into a workflow of LLM inferences, tool invocations, and orchestration decisions that repeatedly cross the CPU-GPU boundary. Our taxonomy explains how this fragmentation turns into resource demand. As orchestration and tools run on the host, the CPU sits on the critical path. Execution structure sets the load over time, which stays low with sudden spikes. Model composition sets how evenly the workflow uses the GPUs. Diversity in tasks and tools widens this range even further. These characteristics expose architectural mismatches of conventional uniform servers. Fragmented execution strands CPU and GPU capacity despite bursty demand. Different software roles make homogeneous CPU provisioning inefficient. Finally, multiplexing many agents onto shared cores degrades microarchitectural locality. Guided by our findings, we derive implications for agentic servers and examine them through Agora, our prototype for commodity servers. Agora dynamically harvests idle CPU cores for co-located throughput work, while protecting agentic tail latency against tool spikes. It oversubscribes GPU memory by placing more agents on each GPU, prefetching the next agent's state to hide swap latency. To match the machine to the heterogeneous roles, Agora pools cores by role and applies affinity-aware scheduling to restore locality. It automatically tunes mechanisms to the workload. Agora improves utilization and server throughput while preserving agent tail latency. Our insights also identify key directions for future server architectures for agentic AI.

</details>


### [119] [CARGO-VL: Counterfactual Arbitration with Risk-Constrained Group Optimization for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04509)
*De Jiang,Zhengyang Zhang,Kehong Yuan,Shaohua Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CARGO-VL提出了一种群组相对（group-relative）的训练框架，通过同时优化四种证据状态（对齐/图像正确/文本正确/双错）的匹配变体，并利用转换奖励和原始-对偶控制器，提升视觉-语言系统在证据冲突下的可靠性，包括正确的来源仲裁和必要时弃权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言系统在图像与检索文本不一致或共同无法支撑答案时缺乏可靠性。已有的后训练目标对样本独立打分，无法在反事实证据变化下强制执行一致行为，导致模型难以判断可信来源或在证据不足时恰当地弃权。

Method: （1）提出CARGO-VL框架，将四种证据状态（对齐A、图像正确V、文本正确T、双错N）的匹配变体作为一个捆绑优化单元；（2）目标函数耦合条件正确性与转换奖励，涵盖答案不变性、来源等变性以及从答案到弃权的切换；（3）采用原始-对偶控制器在"不安全答案"与"过度弃权"之间取得平衡；（4）贡献XMC（eXtended Modal Conflict）四条件冲突训练资源，并在CMC-Bench和Modality-Bias上评估迁移。

Result: 在多个随机种子下，CARGO-VL在冲突处理、不支持答案的规避以及模态平衡方面均优于逐点（pointwise）基线。消融实验表明关系性转换信号与自适应风险控制具有互补收益，验证了反事实一致性作为可靠多模态证据仲裁目标的实用性。

Conclusion: 反事实一致性可作为实用的训练目标来提升多模态证据仲裁的可靠性；CARGO-VL通过群组相对优化、转换奖励与自适应风险控制相结合，使模型在面对证据冲突时能够识别可信来源并在双重不可靠时正确弃权。

Abstract: Vision-language systems combine images with retrieved text, but these sources can disagree or jointly fail to support an answer. Reliable models must identify the trustworthy source and abstain when neither is adequate. Existing post-training objectives score instances independently and therefore do not enforce coherent behavior under counterfactual evidence changes. We introduce CARGO-VL, a group-relative framework that optimizes matched variants covering aligned, image-correct, text-correct, and both-wrong (A/V/T/N) evidence states as one bundle. Its objective couples condition-wise correctness with transition rewards for answer invariance, source equivariance, and answer-to-abstention switching, while a primal-dual controller balances unsafe answers against excessive deferral. We also contribute XMC (eXtended Modal Conflict), a four-condition conflict training resource, and evaluate transfer on CMC-Bench and Modality-Bias. Across multiple seeds, CARGO-VL improves conflict handling, unsupported-answer avoidance, and modality balance over pointwise baselines. Ablations identify complementary benefits from relational transition signals and adaptive risk control, supporting counterfactual consistency as a practical objective for reliable multimodal evidence arbitration.

</details>


### [120] [Leak-Resistant Unlearning: A New Benchmark for Evaluating Multi-Hop Reasoning Consistency and Recovery Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04519)
*Haoting Qian,Qingjie Zhang,Zhicong Huang,Cheng Hong,Han Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个新的基准测试来评估大语言模型（LLM）机器遗忘方法的健壮性，重点关注多样化多跳推理路径和恢复攻击下的知识清除效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遗忘基准主要包含单跳问题和有限的多跳问题，面临两个挑战：(1) 知识不是孤立存在的，多样的多跳推理路径可能比普通查询更容易引发知识泄露；(2) 遗忘可能是脆弱的，遗忘的知识可以通过轻量级后期遗忘适配等恢复攻击被部分恢复，使得静态评估不充分。

Method: 提出一个全新的基准测试框架，包含多样化推理路径和恢复攻击两个维度，在3个模型、6种遗忘方法和2个精心整理的数据集上进行实验评估。

Result: 实验结果表明现有方法在面对多跳推理路径和恢复攻击时是脆弱的。进一步探索了遗忘质量、健壮性和模型效用之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 现有LLM遗忘方法在多跳推理路径和恢复攻击场景下表现脆弱，需要更健壮的遗忘策略来平衡遗忘质量、健壮性和模型效用。

Abstract: Benchmarking machine unlearning methods is critical to understand whether sensitive knowledge is removed from large language models (LLMs) or not. Current unlearning benchmarks include mainly single-hop questions and a narrow set of multi-hop questions. Although effective, they still face two challenges. (1) Knowledge is not isolated, whereby diverse multi-hop reasoning paths can potentially induce knowledge leakage than normal queries. (2) Unlearning may be fragile: unlearned knowledge can be partially recovered through recovery attacks such as lightweight post-unlearning adaptation, making static evaluation insufficient. Therefore, in this paper, we introduce \unlearning as a novel benchmark to understand robust LLM knowledge removal across diverse reasoning paths and recovery attacks. We experiment with this benchmark on 3 models, 6 unlearning methods, and 2 carefully curated datasets. Results show that existing methods are vulnerable to multi-hop reasoning paths and recovery attacks. We further explore the trade-off among forget quality, robustness, and model utility for LLM unlearning.

</details>


### [121] [What Is a Skill Worth? Structure-Aware Shapley Valuation of Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04562)
*Tao Li,Junfeng Liu,Qinghua Zhao,Yifan Li,Lei Wang,Bo Shao,Xuejun Liu,Linjun Shou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillSV，一个结构感知的Shapley式框架，用于评估Agent技能内部单元（规则、示例、脚本、启发式）的价值，通过编译技能为单元、依赖和层次结构来评估有效反事实技能，并使用配对删除与长度中性填充分离内容价值与上下文成本，配合预算Rollout估计器处理噪声评估，在四个Agent基准上验证了其忠实性、可操作性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent技能越来越多地通过自动化反馈循环优化，产生长结构化工件，但其内部单元的实际价值尚不明确。现有的数据或提示跨度估值方法无法处理技能单元的复杂结构，因为技能单元之间存在依赖关系、属于文档层次结构、可触发Agent行为并消耗有限的提示上下文。因此需要专门的结构感知估值方法。

Method: SkillSV框架包含三个关键组件：(1) 将技能编译为单元、依赖关系与层次结构，确保只评估有效的反事实技能；(2) 采用配对删除和长度中性填充来分离内容价值与上下文成本；(3) 使用带Rollout预算的估计器处理噪声Agent评估。

Result: 在四个Agent基准上，SkillSV恢复了单元交互，保持了聚合技能提升，并能指导安全的剪枝和压缩操作。

Conclusion: SkillSV提供了一种结构感知的技能估值方法，不仅能够准确评估技能内部单元的价值，还能在实际应用中指导技能的安全修剪与压缩，提升了技能的可用性和效率。

Abstract: Agent skills are increasingly optimized by automated feedback loops, producing long structured artifacts whose internal value remains unclear. We study skill valuation: assigning credit to the internal units of a fixed skill, such as rules, examples, scripts, and heuristics, under a fixed agent and held-out task distribution. Skill valuation differs from data or prompt-span valuation because skill units are structured: they may depend on other units, belong to a document hierarchy, trigger agent behavior, and consume limited prompt context. We introduce SkillSV, a structure-aware Shapley-style framework for skill valuation. SkillSV compiles a skill into units, dependencies, and hierarchy, so that only valid counterfactual skills are evaluated. It uses paired deletion and length-neutral padding to separate content value from context cost, and estimates the resulting values with a rollout-budgeted estimator for noisy agent evaluations. On four agentic benchmarks, we assess the faithfulness, actionability, and explanation of SkillSV: it recovers unit interactions, preserves aggregate skill lift, and guides safe pruning and compression.

</details>


### [122] [Joint UAV Flight and Opportunistic Routing under Reinforcement Learning for Delay-Tolerant Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04590)
*Xiao Wang,Shun-Ren Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出JUROR框架，通过联合优化分散式机会路由与无人机飞行控制，在延迟容忍网络（DTN）中应对稀疏连接导致的路由与拥塞问题。该框架基于PPO，采用集中式训练-分散式执行（CTDE）架构，将问题建模为因子化POMDP，并引入多视角热点预测器辅助训练，仿真验证了其在多种流量模式下优于PRoPHET和MaxProp。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DTN网络的稀疏连接、有限缓冲和消息TTL限制导致投递率低与拥塞，端到端性能严重退化，亟需联合优化路由决策与UAV可控移动以扩大未来接触机会。

Method: 1) 将问题建模为具有序贯运动-路由耦合与每步团队奖励的因子化POMDP；2) 分散执行者基于局部观测决策，训练期批评者利用全局统计；3) 基于PPO框架实现集中式训练-分散式执行（CTDE）；4) 可选的多视角热点预测器提供辅助监督信号。

Result: 在四种流量模式下的仿真实验中，JUROR相比PRoPHET和MaxProp实现了有效增益，同时保持了接触受限的分散式执行能力。

Conclusion: 联合UAV飞行控制与机会路由优化能够有效缓解DTN的稀疏投递与拥塞问题，CTDE架构在保持分散式可执行性的同时取得了优于传统协议的性能。

Abstract: The growing deployment of delay-tolerant networks (DTNs) has made store-carry-forward (SCF) communication indispensable under sparse connectivity.
  However, intermittent contacts, finite buffers, and limited message time-to-live (TTL) often give rise to sparse delivery and congestion, leading to substantial end-to-end performance degradation.
  To address this challenge, this study explores the joint optimization of decentralized opportunistic routing and controllable unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) flight, aiming to enlarge future contacts through discrete UAV headings while enabling per-node replication under contact-limited observations.
  Building upon this architecture, we study cooperative factored routing--UAV control under centralized training and decentralized execution (CTDE) and propose JUROR (Joint UAV flight and Opportunistic Routing, based on the proximal policy optimization (PPO) framework.
  In our design, we first cast the problem as a factored partially observable Markov decision process with sequential motion--routing coupling and a per-step team reward; subsequently, decentralized actors act on local observations while a training-time critic uses global statistics, and an optional multi-horizon hotspot predictor provides auxiliary supervision.
  Simulation results over four traffic modes demonstrate effective gains over PRoPHET and MaxProp, while retaining contact-limited decentralized execution.

</details>


### [123] [Agreement Before Diversity: Verification-First Complementarity for Heterogeneous Language-Model Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04618)
*Ruitong Li,Binjie Guo,Aisheng Mo,Guowei Su,Jie Li,Ru Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ABD（Agreement-Before-Diversity）方法，将异构语言模型集成中"何时用新回答替换已有回答"的问题形式化为一个冻结的、无标签的决策规则：若两个额外的可信样本在固定等价关系下佐证锚定答案则保留，否则由异构综合替换。作者证明了两条精确恒等式，量化了该门控机制相对无条件综合和永不合成的准确率差距，并在LiveCodeBench-v6和GPQA-Diamond上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构语言模型集成扩大了候选回答空间，但缺乏一个原则性的准则来判断新生成的回答何时应该取代已被支持的现有回答。现有方法未将候选"潜力"与"替换权限"解耦，导致替换决策缺乏可审计性和理论依据。

Method: ABD方法是一个冻结的、无需标签的决策规则：若两个额外可信样本在固定等价关系下佐证锚点回答则保留锚点；否则采用异构合成回答。该方法将候选多样性（headroom）与替换权威（authority）解耦，使后者成为显式、可审计的对象。作者证明了两条恒等式：第一条表明相对无条件合成的准确率差距由一致覆盖率和锚点在被保护子集上的优势共同决定；第二条表明相对永不合成的差距反映"授权恢复"与"授权破坏"之间的对比。两条恒等式均不假设独立性或校准置信度，期望推理成本约为8-5×覆盖率次调用。

Result: 在盲测、精确ID评估下，ABD在完整LiveCodeBench-v6上达到59.43%（对比Single9的52.57%和HAC的52.00%，n=175），在未触及的GPQA-Diamond分割上达到75.00%（两个对照组均为72.78%，n=180）。所证明的恒等式将所有聚合差异定位到可枚举的受保护层：LiveCodeBench上3个受保护案例中无不一致项（覆盖率先验地将门控贡献限制在1.71分内）；GPQA-Diamond上132例中有13例对8例不一致；冻结锚点扰动下71例中有12例对0例不一致。

Conclusion: 多样性提供潜力，验证结构提供权威。ABD通过将替换决策转变为可审计的、有理论保证的门控机制，实现了异构集成中"何时合成"这一核心问题的形式化解决，且推理成本可控，具备黑盒可部署性。

Abstract: Heterogeneous language-model ensembles expand the space of candidate responses, yet they lack a principled criterion for when a newly generated answer should supersede an already supported one. We decouple candidate headroom from replacement authority, rendering the latter as an explicit, auditable object. Our proposed method, Agreement-Before-Diversity (ABD), is a frozen, label-free decision rule: an anchor answer is retained if two additional trusted samples corroborate it under a fixed equivalence relation; otherwise, it is replaced by a heterogeneous synthesis. For this gating mechanism, we prove two exact identities. The first shows that the accuracy gap relative to unconditional synthesis is determined jointly by the agreement coverage and the anchor's advantage on the protected subset. The second shows that the gap relative to never synthesizing reflects a contrast between authorized recovery and authorized destruction. Neither identity assumes independence or calibrated confidence, and the expected inference cost is approximately eight minus five times the coverage in number of calls. Under blind, exact-ID evaluation, ABD achieves 59.43% on the complete LiveCodeBench-v6 (vs. 52.57% for Single9 and 52.00% for HAC; n = 175) and 75.00% on an untouched GPQA-Diamond split (both controls at 72.78%; n = 180). Furthermore, these identities localize every aggregate difference to an enumerable protected stratum: no discordant items occur among the 3 protected cases on LiveCodeBench, where coverage bounds the gate's contribution to 1.71 points a priori; 13 versus 8 discordant cases among 132 on GPQA-Diamond; and 12 versus 0 among 71 under a frozen anchor perturbation. Diversity supplies potential; verification structure supplies authority.

</details>


### [124] [A/B Agent: A Self-Evolving Agent for Strategy Iteration in Industrial A/B Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04625)
*Zhuohang Jiang,Yuxin Chen,Yongsen Pan,Zheng Hu,Wenqi Fan,Qing Li,Hongyang Wang,Jun Wang,Wenwu Ou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出A/B Agent，一个面向工业推荐系统策略优化的闭环智能体框架，通过层级经验树组织历史策略、多路径Tree-RAG检索可迁移证据生成可执行策略，并利用在线A/B反馈驱动自主调优与经验树自进化。在真实短视频电商推荐系统中实现了GMV提升4.829%且所有护栏指标均为正增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统工业推荐策略迭代依赖专家手动设计实验和调参，过程劳动密集且耗时；历史实验知识碎片化，难以系统性复用；现有RAG智能体以扁平方式组织经验，忽略业务场景、推荐阶段、优化目标和实验上下文之间的层级关系，导致检索不匹配、跨场景迁移受限，且无法通过序列化A/B反馈持续优化策略与参数。

Method: 提出A/B Agent闭环框架，包含三个紧密耦合的核心组件：(1) 历史策略知识组织——构建层级经验树；(2) 自主目标感知策略生成——通过多路径Tree-RAG检索可迁移证据并生成可执行策略；(3) 实验引导的策略自进化——持续分析在线A/B反馈，驱动自主调参并更新经验树。

Result: 离线与在线评估均验证了有效性：在真实短视频电商推荐系统中，GMV提升4.829%，且所有护栏指标均保持正增益。

Conclusion: A/B Agent通过层级化的经验组织与闭环的自进化机制，有效解决了工业推荐系统中策略迭代的专家依赖和知识复用难题，实现了显著的业务指标提升与系统稳定性保障。

Abstract: Industrial recommendation strategy iteration heavily relies on large-scale A/B experimentation. Traditional tuning requires experts to repeatedly design strategies, configure experiments, analyze results, and adjust parameters, making the process labor-intensive and time-consuming. Meanwhile, valuable knowledge from historical experiments is often fragmented, making systematic reuse difficult through manual expert effort alone. Existing RAG agents partially alleviate this burden by retrieving prior strategies, but typically organize experience in a flat manner, overlooking the hierarchical relationships among business scenarios, recommendation stages, optimization objectives, and experimental contexts. This often results in mismatched retrieval and limited cross-scenario transfer, while preventing agents from continuously refining strategies and parameters through sequential A/B feedback. %
To address these limitations, we propose A/B Agent, a closed-loop A/B agent for industrial recommendation strategy optimization. The framework comprises three tightly coupled core components: Historical Strategy Knowledge Organization, Autonomous Target-Aware Strategy Generation, and Experiment-Guided Strategy Self-Evolution. It organizes historical strategies into a hierarchical experience tree, retrieves transferable evidence through multi-path Tree-RAG to generate executable strategies, and continuously analyzes online A/B feedback to guide autonomous tuning and update the experience tree for self-evolution. Extensive offline and online evaluations demonstrate its effectiveness, including a 4.829% improvement in GMV in a real-world short-video e-commerce recommendation system while maintaining positive gains across all guardrail metrics.

</details>


### [125] [AI Literacy for Legal Translation: Developing Digital Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04641)
*Łucja Biel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨生成式AI对专业法律翻译的影响，提出了一个针对法律翻译职业的AI素养框架，包含基础性、程序性、批判性和战略性四个相互强化的维度，将AI素养视为法律翻译能力的横向组成要素，以培养数字化韧性，并讨论了该框架在翻译教育中的教学应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI正在改变法律翻译领域，带来了语言、技术、法律、伦理和认知等多方面的风险与机遇，但AI并未改变法律翻译的根本目标。职业法律译者需要适应这一变化，因此有必要提出一个专门的AI素养框架，以帮助从业者批判性地、负责任地集成AI工具，同时保持法律翻译的专业标准。

Method: 本文采用概念分析与理论建构的方法，提出了一个包含四个相互强化维度（基础性、程序性、批判性、战略性）的AI素养框架，将AI素养概念化为法律翻译能力的横向组成部分，并在此基础上讨论该框架的教学意义，设计用于法律翻译教育的课堂活动。

Result: 论文提出并论证了一个四维AI素养框架，将其定位为法律翻译能力中促进数字化韧性的横向要素，并给出了如何在法律翻译教育中通过课堂活动培养AI素养的具体建议，以帮助未来译者在遵循职业标准的前提下批判性地整合AI。

Conclusion: AI并未改变法律翻译的基本目标，但要求专业能力的延伸。通过四维AI素养框架及其教学应用，法律翻译从业者和学生可以发展出批判、负责地使用AI的能力，从而在数字化时代保持专业竞争力和韧性。

Abstract: Generative AI is transforming legal translation by introducing opportunities alongside linguistic, technical, legal, ethical and cognitive risks. This chapter examines the implications of AI for professional legal translation and proposes an AI literacy framework tailored to the profession. It argues that AI does not change the fundamental objectives of legal translation but requires an extension of professional competence through AI literacy. The proposed framework comprises four mutually reinforcing dimensions, foundational, procedural, critical and strategic, and conceptualises AI literacy as a transversal component of legal translation competence that fosters digital resilience. It further discusses the pedagogical implications of this framework by proposing classroom activities designed to develop AI literacy in legal translator education, enabling future translators to integrate AI critically, responsibly and in accordance with professional standards.

</details>


### [126] [Calibrating Artificial Guilt: Neurally Grounded Reward Shaping for Prosocial Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04663)
*Aaditya Mehta,Arya Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究利用人类神经和行为数据校准多智能体强化学习中的内疚信号权重，将人类fMRI数据中提取的愧疚权重嵌入AI代理的奖励塑形中，使代理行为更接近人类的社会决策模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体强化学习中，向个体奖励添加社会性项（如内疚信号）时，其权重通常由人工凭经验设定，缺乏客观依据。作者希望探究能否从人类神经和行为数据中校准这一权重，并将其迁移到人工代理中，以替代手动调参。

Method: 使用SoDec责任fMRI数据集（40名参与者），通过受试者固定效应回归将瞬时幸福感变化对结果类型计数进行拟合，以内疚权重定义为"伴侣负面减去社会负面"的对比度（ŵ=1.118, Cohen's d=0.214）。将该权重嵌入双代理Social Lottery环境，训练独立PPO actor-critic，比较四种奖励塑形方案：神经校准、均匀常数、零（自私）以及单位系数oracle。

Result: 在每种条件下各评估1,000个回合：神经校准代理的社会安全选择率（0.459）最接近人类（0.484），KL散度仅为0.0012；其他三种条件的KL散度偏差大一到三个数量级。

Conclusion: 人类神经行为先验可以作为一种量化的约束条件用于亲社会奖励塑形，为多智能体强化学习中的社会项权重选择提供了数据驱动的替代方案。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning often adds social terms to individual rewards, yet the scale of those terms is usually chosen by hand. We ask whether a guilt signal can instead be calibrated from human neural and behavioural data and transferred to artificial agents. Using the public SoDec responsibility fMRI dataset (40 participants), we fit a subject-fixed-effects regression of momentary-happiness changes on outcome-type counts and recover a guilt weight as the Partner-negative minus Social-negative contrast ($\hat{w}=1.118$, Cohen's $d=0.214$). We embed this weight in a two-agent Social Lottery environment and train independent Proximal Policy Optimization actor-critics under four shaping regimes: neurally calibrated, uniform constant, zero (selfish), and a unit-coefficient oracle. Across 1{,}000 evaluation episodes per condition, the calibrated agents track the human Social safe-choice rate most closely ($0.459$ vs.\ human $0.484$; $\mathrm{KL}=0.0012$), while the other three conditions deviate by one to three orders of magnitude in KL. Human neurobehavioural priors can therefore act as quantitative constraints on prosocial reward shaping.

</details>


### [127] [Traceable LLM-Generated Hazard Scenarios for Operational Safety Analysis of Aviation Systems Using ASRS Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04697)
*Cristian Mascia,Roberto Pietrantuono,Daniel Rodriguez,Stefano Russo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种AI辅助方法，从NASA航空安全报告系统（ASRS）中自动生成候选危险情景，用于运行级航空系统危险分析。方法包括构建结构化因果假设和叙事化事件场景，并提供历史可行性评分与可追溯性。同时提出混合变体，通过进化溯因生成结构化假设来条件化叙事生成，提升正确性并降低变异性。研究评估了多种大语言模型、提示策略与微调的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空系统运行级危险分析必须考虑天气、空中交通管制、空域约束、飞机运行与人因之间的复杂交互，这与飞机系统层面的功能危险评估不同。传统方法难以系统性地生成覆盖这些多维交互的候选危险场景，因此需要自动化辅助手段。

Method: 从ASRS数据出发，针对目标不良后果生成结构化假设（分类因素）和叙事化事件场景。叙事场景与结构化假设保持一致。提出混合变体，利用进化溯因（evolutionary abduction）生成结构化假设，再以此条件化叙事生成。评估中比较多种大语言模型、零样本与少样本提示以及可选微调对生成有效性和真实性的影响。

Result: 混合方法（进化溯因+条件化叙事生成）相比基线在生成正确性上有提升，同时降低了输出变异性。提示策略与模型选择对生成结构与叙事的有效性和真实性均有显著影响。

Conclusion: AI辅助生成候选危险情景可有效支持航空系统运行级的操作危险分析，通过结合结构化推理与叙事生成，能提供可行且可追溯的假设场景，并作为危险识别流程的输入支持。

Abstract: Operational hazard analysis of aviation system operations must consider interactions among weather, ATC actions, airspace constraints, aircraft operations, and human factors - distinct from the functional hazard assessment applied at the aircraft-system level. We present an AI-assisted approach that generates candidate hazard scenarios from NASA's Aviation Safety Reporting System (ASRS). Given a target adverse outcome, it produces a structured hypothesis as categorical factors and a narrative scenario describing an operational event sequence consistent with the structure. Each scenario includes by a plausibility score from historical co-occurrence evidence and traceability to the most similar held-out ASRS reports. We then propose a hybrid variant, conditioning narrative generation on a structured hypothesis produced via evolutionary abduction, improving correctness and reducing variability. We evaluate multiple large language models, zero-shot versus few-shot prompting, and optional fine-tuning, measuring how prompting and model choice affect the validity and realism of the generated structures and narratives.

</details>


### [128] [Diagnosing Tool-Selection Reasoning in LLM Agents with Canary Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04719)
*Atul Anand,Sourav Chattaraj*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"金丝雀工具"（canary tools）：在智能体的 MCP 工具集中植入诊断性探针工具，以精准定位模型在工具选择中的具体弱点。作者设计了六类工具选择缺陷分类法（语义诱饵、参数陷阱、能力幻象、前置条件盲区、时间诱饵、粒度陷阱），将"选错工具"这一单一结果转化为多维推理画像。在三个能力层级的八个模型（六个托管、两个 8B 开放权重模型）上进行了 8,640 次运行（外加 2,880 次消融）。主要发现：①模型越强，易感性越低（跨模型约 36 倍差异）；②能力层级不能预测安全性（最易感托管模型居中档）；③分类法具有能力分层性——能力幻象最易捕获前沿模型，其余类型对强模型基本无效但能触发小型开放模型。软化解密短语后前沿模型 CSR 基本不变，证明测的是推理而非短语识别。易感性与任务失败相关（Spearman rho=-0.34），最稳健的模型不受金丝雀压力显著影响。框架、schema、任务、日志均已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估只能告诉我们模型选错了工具，却无法解释"为什么"选错。作者希望将粗粒度的错误信号分解为细粒度的、可诊断的工具选择弱点画像，从而为模型开发和部署提供可操作的改进方向。

Method: 设计六类金丝雀探针工具（语义诱饵、参数陷阱、能力幻象、前置条件盲区、时间诱饵、粒度陷阱），植入智能体 MCP 工具集；在 120 个任务、三种金丝雀密度条件、三种随机种子下，对八个模型（三个能力层级）进行系统评测（共 8,640 次运行加 2,880 次消融）；采用供应商无关的裁判（辅以第二独立裁判，Cohen's kappa=0.75）打分；通过软化解密短语的消融实验验证探针测量的是推理而非短语匹配。
